**(d) 在 `describe` 之前插入四个供给场景方法**：

```js
  /* ---------------- P4：供给场景（离线批处理） ---------------- */

  /** 供给场景要写 learning 仓；没接上就报 503 */
  function requireUnitsDir() {
    if (!unitsDir) throw serviceUnavailable("没连上 learning 仓，写入不可用")
    return unitsDir
  }

  /** 批次的成本护栏（会话桶 + 每日桶，批次 id 当会话 id 用） */
  function ensureBatchQuota(batchId) {
    const quota = checkBatch(store, batchId, dateStr(now()))
    if (!quota.ok) throw quotaError(quota.message)
    return quota
  }

  /** 供给场景的单元上下文：从单元 + learning 仓里的卡片文件拼出来 */
  function supplyContext(unit, extra) {
    const ctx = {
      title: String((unit && unit.title) || ""),
      difficulty: String((unit && unit.difficulty) || "medium"),
      topics:
        Array.isArray(unit && unit.topics) && unit.topics.length
          ? unit.topics.join("、")
          : "未标注",
      statement: String((unit && unit.statement) || ""),
      demo: "",
      readme: "",
      notes: "",
      question: "",
    }
    const demoRel = String((unit && unit.entry && unit.entry.demo) || "")
    if (learningRoot && demoRel) {
      const demoAbs = path.join(learningRoot, demoRel)
      ctx.demo = readTextOrEmpty(demoAbs)
      const dir = path.dirname(demoAbs)
      ctx.readme = readTextOrEmpty(path.join(dir, "README.md"))
      ctx.notes = readTextOrEmpty(path.join(dir, "NOTES.md"))
    }
    if (extra) {
      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
        if (extra[key] !== undefined && extra[key] !== null) {
          ctx[key] = String(extra[key])
        }
      })
    }
    return ctx
  }

  /** 卡片单元的 frontmatter 字段 */
  function cardUnitFields(unit, patch) {
    const p = patch || {}
    const entry = (unit && unit.entry) || {}
    const stem = stemOf(unit)
    return {
      id: unit.id,
      track: unit.track,
      kind: "card",
      title: String(p.title || unit.title || ""),
      difficulty: String(p.difficulty || unit.difficulty || "medium"),
      topics: Array.isArray(p.topics) ? p.topics : (Array.isArray(unit.topics) ? unit.topics : []),
      source: "generated",
      language: "python",
      demo: String(entry.demo || ""),
      reference: "",
      graded: "script",
      script: String(
        entry.script || path.join("units", unit.track, stem + ".assert.py").replace(/\\/g, "/")
      ),
      status: "draft",
      reviewed: false,
      reviewed_at: "",
      subtracks: Array.isArray(unit.subtracks) ? unit.subtracks : [],
    }
  }

  /** 单元文件不存在就建一个（已存在就原样留着，不覆盖） */
  function ensureUnitFile(unit, patch) {
    const dir = requireUnitsDir()
    const file = unitFilePath(dir, unit)
    if (fs.existsSync(file)) return file
    const fields = cardUnitFields(unit, patch)
    const demoRel = String(((unit.entry && unit.entry.demo) || ""))
    const cardDir = demoRel ? path.posix.dirname(demoRel.split(path.sep).join("/")) : ""
    const related = cardDir ? ["- 卡片目录：" + cardDir] : []
    const content = renderUnitFile(fields, { explain: "", related: related })
    const written = writeFileChecked(file, content, { repoRoot: learningRoot, stateFile: stateFile })
    if (!written.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写单元文件失败：" + written.reason)
    return file
  }

  /**
   * 生成断言脚本（DB/AI 卡）。
   * @param {{unit: object, batchId?: string, sessionId?: string, context?: object}} req
   * @returns {Promise<{unit: object, script: string, topics: string[], model: string, file: string, unitFile: string}>}
   */
  async function generateAssert(req) {
    const r = req || {}
    const unit = r.unit
    if (!unit) throw badRequest("缺少单元")
    if (!hasSupplyTemplate(unit.track, "generate-assert")) {
      throw badRequest("轨道「" + String(unit.track) + "」不生成断言脚本")
    }
    const client = currentClient()
    if (client.name === "null") throw serviceUnavailable(offMessage(client))
    const batchId = r.batchId || r.sessionId || null
    ensureBatchQuota(batchId)

    const prompt = buildSupplyPrompt(
      "generate-assert",
      unit.track,
      supplyContext(unit, r.context)
    )
    const raw = await client.complete({ system: prompt.system, user: prompt.user, temperature: 0.2 })
    const parsed = parseSupplyResponse(raw, "generate-assert")
    if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error("生成断言脚本失败：" + parsed.reason)
    consumeBatch(store, batchId, dateStr(now()))
    recordTrace(unit.id, "generate-assert", "", client.model, prompt)

    const dir = requireUnitsDir()
    const unitFile = ensureUnitFile(unit, { topics: parsed.topics })
    const scriptFile = assertFilePath(dir, unit)
    const written = writeFileChecked(scriptFile, parsed.script + "\n", {
      repoRoot: learningRoot,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    if (!written.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写断言脚本失败：" + written.reason)
    return {
      unit: unit,
      script: parsed.script,
      topics: parsed.topics,
      model: client.model,
      file: scriptFile,
      unitFile: unitFile,
    }
  }

  /**
   * 生成讲解正文，写进单元文件的「## 讲解」小节。
   * @param {{unit: object, batchId?: string, sessionId?: string, context?: object}} req
   * @returns {Promise<{unit: object, explain: string, model: string, file: string}>}
   */
  async function writeExplain(req) {
    const r = req || {}
    const unit = r.unit
    if (!unit) throw badRequest("缺少单元")
    if (!hasSupplyTemplate(unit.track, "write-explain")) {
      throw badRequest("轨道「" + String(unit.track) + "」没有讲解正文模板")
    }
    const client = currentClient()
    if (client.name === "null") throw serviceUnavailable(offMessage(client))
    const batchId = r.batchId || r.sessionId || null
    ensureBatchQuota(batchId)

    const prompt = buildSupplyPrompt(
      "write-explain",
      unit.track,
      supplyContext(unit, r.context)
    )
    const raw = await client.complete({ system: prompt.system, user: prompt.user, temperature: 0.4 })
    const parsed = parseSupplyResponse(raw, "write-explain")
    if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error("生成讲解正文失败：" + parsed.reason)
    consumeBatch(store, batchId, dateStr(now()))
    recordTrace(unit.id, "write-explain", "", client.model, prompt)

    const dir = requireUnitsDir()
    const unitFile = unitFilePath(dir, unit)
    if (!fs.existsSync(unitFile)) {
      throw badRequest("单元文件还不存在，先跑 generate 生成断言脚本")
    }
    const before = fs.readFileSync(unitFile, "utf8")
    const after = replaceSection(before, "讲解", parsed.text)
    const written = writeFileChecked(unitFile, after, {
      repoRoot: learningRoot,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    if (!written.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写讲解正文失败：" + written.reason)
    return { unit: unit, explain: parsed.text, model: client.model, file: unitFile }
  }

  /**
   * 深挖概念：把一段更深的说明追加到「## 讲解」小节末尾。
   * @param {{unit: object, batchId?: string, sessionId?: string, question?: string, context?: object}} req
   * @returns {Promise<{unit: object, text: string, model: string, file: string}>}
   */
  async function deepen(req) {
    const r = req || {}
    const unit = r.unit
    if (!unit) throw badRequest("缺少单元")
    if (!hasSupplyTemplate(unit.track, "deepen")) {
      throw badRequest("轨道「" + String(unit.track) + "」没有深挖模板")
    }
    const client = currentClient()
    if (client.name === "null") throw serviceUnavailable(offMessage(client))
    const batchId = r.batchId || r.sessionId || null
    ensureBatchQuota(batchId)

    const question = String(r.question || "").trim()
    const extra = Object.assign({}, r.context || {})
    if (question) extra.question = question
    const prompt = buildSupplyPrompt("deepen", unit.track, supplyContext(unit, extra))
    const raw = await client.complete({ system: prompt.system, user: prompt.user, temperature: 0.4 })
    const parsed = parseSupplyResponse(raw, "deepen")
    if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error("深挖概念失败：" + parsed.reason)
    consumeBatch(store, batchId, dateStr(now()))
    recordTrace(unit.id, "deepen", question, client.model, prompt)

    const dir = requireUnitsDir()
    const unitFile = unitFilePath(dir, unit)
    if (!fs.existsSync(unitFile)) {
      throw badRequest("单元文件还不存在，先跑 generate 生成断言脚本")
    }
    const before = fs.readFileSync(unitFile, "utf8")
    const heading = "### 深挖 · " + (question || "再挖一层")
    const after = appendToSection(before, "讲解", heading + "\n\n" + parsed.text)
    const written = writeFileChecked(unitFile, after, {
      repoRoot: learningRoot,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    if (!written.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写深挖段落失败：" + written.reason)
    return { unit: unit, text: parsed.text, model: client.model, file: unitFile }
  }

  /**
   * 生成算法题单元（题面 + 用例 + 参考解法）。
   * @param {{unit: object, batchId?: string, sessionId?: string, context?: object}} req
   * @returns {Promise<{unit: object, generated: object, model: string, file: string, referenceFile: string}>}
   */
  async function generateUnit(req) {
    const r = req || {}
    const unit = r.unit
    if (!unit) throw badRequest("缺少单元")
    if (!hasSupplyTemplate(unit.track, "generate-unit")) {
      throw badRequest("轨道「" + String(unit.track) + "」不生成题面")
    }
    const client = currentClient()
    if (client.name === "null") throw serviceUnavailable(offMessage(client))
    const batchId = r.batchId || r.sessionId || null
    ensureBatchQuota(batchId)

    const prompt = buildSupplyPrompt(
      "generate-unit",
      unit.track,
      supplyContext(unit, r.context)
    )
    const raw = await client.complete({ system: prompt.system, user: prompt.user, temperature: 0.8 })
    const parsed = parseSupplyResponse(raw, "generate-unit")
    if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error("生成单元失败：" + parsed.reason)
    consumeBatch(store, batchId, dateStr(now()))
    recordTrace(unit.id, "generate-unit", "", client.model, prompt)

    const dir = requireUnitsDir()
    const stem = stemOf(unit)
    const lang = parsed.unit.solutionLang === "python" ? "py" : "cpp"
    const referenceFile = path.join(dir, unit.track, stem + ".reference." + lang)
    const writtenSolution = writeFileChecked(referenceFile, parsed.unit.solution + "\n", {
      repoRoot: learningRoot,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    if (!writtenSolution.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写参考解法失败：" + writtenSolution.reason)

    const fields = {
      id: unit.id,
      track: unit.track,
      kind: "problem",
      title: parsed.unit.title,
      difficulty: parsed.unit.difficulty,
      topics: parsed.unit.topics,
      source: "generated",
      language: lang === "py" ? "python" : "cpp",
      demo: "",
      reference: path.join("units", unit.track, stem + ".reference." + lang).replace(/\\/g, "/"),
      graded: "user",
      script: "",
      status: "draft",
      reviewed: false,
      reviewed_at: "",
      subtracks: Array.isArray(unit.subtracks) ? unit.subtracks : [],
    }
    const unitFile = unitFilePath(dir, unit)
    const content =
      renderUnitFile(fields, { explain: parsed.unit.statement, related: [] }) +
      "\n## 用例\n\n" +
      casesTable(parsed.unit.cases) +
      "\n"
    const written = writeFileChecked(unitFile, content, {
      repoRoot: learningRoot,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    if (!written.ok) throw serviceUnavailable("写单元文件失败：" + written.reason)
    return {
      unit: unit,
      generated: parsed.unit,
      model: client.model,
      file: unitFile,
      referenceFile: referenceFile,
    }
  }
```

### Task 6: 缺口扫描（`core/supply/plan.js`）

**Files:**
- 新建 `core/supply/plan.js`
- 新建 `tests/supply-plan.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 契约 §8.4（`GET /api/supply/status` 的响应形状，`missing` 取值域 `["asserts","statement","cases","solution"]`）、§10（`plan` 子命令：扫描三轨 → 输出缺口清单 → 同时写 `learning/units/<track>/plan.json`）、§3.3（loader C 的 `## 讲解` 小节）、§3.4（`loadUnits` 去重后 C 优先）、§14.1（卡的轨道归属）
- Produces:
  - `GAP_KINDS : string[]` —— `["asserts", "statement", "cases", "solution"]`
  - `EMPTY_EXPLAIN : string` —— loader C 单元文件里讲解未生成的占位符
  - `scanGaps(opts) : {tracks: Array<{track,total,todo,draft,verified,rejected,reviewed}>, gaps: Array<{id,track,kind,title,missing:string[]}>, warnings: string[]}`
  - `writePlanFiles(opts) : {written: string[], blocked: string[], reason: string}`
  - `missingOf(unit, opts) : string[]`

**口径**（四条规则，逐轨不同）：
- **`kind: "card"`（DB/AI 卡）**：缺 **断言脚本** → `missing` 含 `"asserts"`（判据：`entry.script` 指向的文件在 learning 仓里不存在）；讲解正文是空的或还是占位符 → `missing` 含 `"statement"`。
- **`kind: "problem"`（算法题）**：题面为空 → `"statement"`；断言集为空（即测试用例为空）→ `"cases"`；参考解法为空 → `"solution"`。
- 计数里的 `todo/draft/verified/rejected` 按 `unit.status` 分桶；`reviewed` 数的是 `unit.reviewed === true` 的单元。
- `gaps` 按 `id` 字符串升序（确定性）。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败测试**

新建 `tests/supply-plan.test.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply-plan.test.js —— 供给缺口扫描
 *
 * 口径（见计划 Task 6）：
 *   卡：缺断言脚本 → "asserts"；讲解是占位符 → "statement"
 *   题：题面空 → "statement"；用例空 → "cases"；参考解法空 → "solution"
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")

const {
  scanGaps,
  writePlanFiles,
  missingOf,
  GAP_KINDS,
  EMPTY_EXPLAIN,
} = require("../core/supply/plan.js")

/** 造一张卡单元 */
function cardUnit(patch) {
  const p = patch || {}
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: "db:mvcc",
      track: "database",
      kind: "card",
      title: "MVCC",
      statement: "# MVCC\n\n## 要点\n版本链\n",
      entry: {
        demo: "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py",
        reference: "",
        graded: "script",
        script: "units/database/mvcc.assert.py",
        language: "python",
      },
      asserts: [],
      topics: [],
      difficulty: "medium",
      source: "grokking",
      status: "verified",
      progress: "todo",
      reviewed: false,
      reviewed_at: "",
      subtracks: [],
      path: "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/",
    },
    p
  )
}

/** 造一道算法题单元 */
function problemUnit(patch) {
  const p = patch || {}
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: "algo:lc-00007",
      track: "algorithm",
      kind: "problem",
      title: "某道题",
      statement: "题面在这里",
      entry: {
        demo: "",
        reference: "",
        graded: "user",
        script: "",
        language: "cpp",
      },
      asserts: [{ type: "output", input: "1 2", expect: "3", cmp: "exact" }],
      topics: [],
      difficulty: "medium",
      source: "leetcode",
      status: "verified",
      progress: "todo",
      reviewed: false,
      reviewed_at: "",
      subtracks: [],
      path: "bank/fundamentals/lc-00007.md",
    },
    p
  )
}

/** 造一个假的 learning 仓：只有「文件在不在」这一件事是真的 */
function makeRoot(files) {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-plan-"))
  Object.keys(files || {}).forEach(function (rel) {
    const abs = path.join(root, rel.split("/").join(path.sep))
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true })
    fs.writeFileSync(abs, files[rel], "utf8")
  })
  return root
}

test("缺口种类与契约 §8.4 一致", function () {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(GAP_KINDS, ["asserts", "statement", "cases", "solution"])
})

test("卡缺断言脚本 → missing 含 asserts", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({ "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py": "print(1)\n" })
  const missing = missingOf(cardUnit(), { learningRoot: root })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(missing, ["asserts"])
})

test("卡有断言脚本但讲解还是占位符 → missing 含 statement", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({
    "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py": "print(1)\n",
    "units/database/mvcc.assert.py": "print(1)\n",
  })
  const unit = cardUnit({ statement: EMPTY_EXPLAIN })
  const missing = missingOf(unit, { learningRoot: root })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(missing, ["statement"])
})

test("卡齐了 → 没有缺口", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({
    "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py": "print(1)\n",
    "units/database/mvcc.assert.py": "print(1)\n",
  })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(missingOf(cardUnit(), { learningRoot: root }), [])
})

test("算法题：题面空 → statement；用例空 → cases；参考解法空 → solution", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({})
  const unit = problemUnit({ statement: "", asserts: [] })
  const missing = missingOf(unit, { learningRoot: root, contentRoots: [root] })
  assert.ok(missing.indexOf("statement") !== -1, missing.join(","))
  assert.ok(missing.indexOf("cases") !== -1, missing.join(","))
  assert.ok(missing.indexOf("solution") !== -1, missing.join(","))
})

test("算法题：读得到原文件且参考解法非空 → 不报 solution", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({
    "bank/fundamentals/lc-00007.md":
      "---\nid: lc-00007\n---\n\n## 题面\n\n题面\n\n## 测试用例\n\n| input | expect |\n|---|---|\n| 1 2 | 3 |\n\n## 参考解法\n\n```cpp\nint main(){}\n```\n",
  })
  const unit = problemUnit()
  const missing = missingOf(unit, { learningRoot: root, contentRoots: [root] })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(missing, [])
})

test("scanGaps 的轨道计数正确", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({
    "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py": "print(1)\n",
    "units/database/mvcc.assert.py": "print(1)\n",
  })
  const units = [
    cardUnit({ id: "db:mvcc", status: "verified", reviewed: true }),
    cardUnit({ id: "db:transaction", status: "draft", entry: { demo: "scaffold/grokking/transaction/main.py", reference: "", graded: "script", script: "units/database/transaction.assert.py", language: "python" } }),
    problemUnit({ id: "algo:lc-00007", status: "todo" }),
  ]
  const result = scanGaps({ units: units, learningRoot: root, contentRoots: [root] })
  const db = result.tracks.filter(function (t) { return t.track === "database" })[0]
  assert.strictEqual(db.total, 2)
  assert.strictEqual(db.verified, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(db.draft, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(db.reviewed, 1)
  const algo = result.tracks.filter(function (t) { return t.track === "algorithm" })[0]
  assert.strictEqual(algo.total, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(algo.todo, 1)
  assert.ok(result.tracks.length === 3, "三条轨都要出现")
})

test("scanGaps 的 gaps 按 id 升序，且能按轨过滤", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({})
  const units = [
    cardUnit({ id: "db:zeta" }),
    cardUnit({ id: "db:alpha" }),
    problemUnit({ id: "algo:lc-00007", statement: "" }),
  ]
  const all = scanGaps({ units: units, learningRoot: root, contentRoots: [root] })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(
    all.gaps.map(function (g) { return g.id }),
    ["algo:lc-00007", "db:alpha", "db:zeta"]
  )
  const onlyDb = scanGaps({ units: units, learningRoot: root, contentRoots: [root], track: "database" })
  assert.strictEqual(onlyDb.tracks.length, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(onlyDb.tracks[0].track, "database")
  assert.deepStrictEqual(
    onlyDb.gaps.map(function (g) { return g.id }),
    ["db:alpha", "db:zeta"]
  )
})

test("writePlanFiles 把缺口清单写进 learning/units/<track>/plan.json", function () {
  const root = makeRoot({
    "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py": "print(1)\n",
  })
  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units")
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database"), { recursive: true })
  const result = scanGaps({
    units: [cardUnit({ id: "db:mvcc" })],
    learningRoot: root,
    contentRoots: [root],
  })
  const written = writePlanFiles({
    result: result,
    unitsDir: unitsDir,
    repoRoot: root,
    stateFile: null,
  })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(written.blocked, [])
  const file = path.join(unitsDir, "database", "plan.json")
  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(file), "plan.json 要落盘")
  const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"))
  assert.strictEqual(parsed.track, "database")
  assert.strictEqual(parsed.gaps.length, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(parsed.gaps[0].id, "db:mvcc")
  assert.deepStrictEqual(parsed.gaps[0].missing, ["asserts"])
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3：写最小实现**

新建 `core/supply/plan.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * plan.js —— 供给缺口扫描
 *
 * 回答两个问题：
 *   1. 每轨现在有多少能学的（todo/draft/verified/rejected）、多少抽检过（reviewed）；
 *   2. 每个单元还欠什么（断言脚本 / 讲解正文 / 测试用例 / 参考解法）。
 *
 * 缺口种类与契约 §8.4 的 missing 取值域一一对应：
 *   "asserts"   —— 断言脚本不存在（卡）
 *   "statement" —— 讲解正文/题面为空（卡 / 题）
 *   "cases"     —— 测试用例为空（题）
 *   "solution"  —— 参考解法为空（题）
 *
 * 卡与题的判据不同：
 *   卡（kind=card）  断言在**独立脚本**里，判据是脚本文件在不在；
 *   题（kind=problem）断言就是测试用例，判据是 unit.asserts 空不空。
 */

const fs = require("node:fs")
const path = require("node:path")

const { TRACK_CODES } = require("../track/tracks.js")
const { parseProblemMarkdown } = require("../bank/parse.js")

/** 缺口种类（契约 §8.4） */
const GAP_KINDS = ["asserts", "statement", "cases", "solution"]

/** loader C 单元文件里「讲解还没生成」的占位符 */
const EMPTY_EXPLAIN = "（讲解正文还没生成）"

/**
 * 单元文件相对 learning 仓根的路径 → 绝对路径。
 * @param {string} root
 * @param {string} rel
 * @returns {string}
 */
function absOf(root, rel) {
  return path.join(root, String(rel).split("/").join(path.sep))
}

/**
 * 断言脚本在不在。
 * @param {object} unit
 * @param {string|null} learningRoot
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function hasAssertScript(unit, learningRoot) {
  const script = String((unit.entry && unit.entry.script) || "")
  if (script === "" || !learningRoot) return false
  return fs.existsSync(absOf(learningRoot, script))
}

/**
 * 读一道题的参考解法。
 * @param {object} unit
 * @param {string[]} contentRoots
 * @returns {string|null} 解法正文；null 表示**读不到原文件，判断不了**
 */
function readSolution(unit, contentRoots) {
  const list = Array.isArray(contentRoots) ? contentRoots : []
  for (const root of list) {
    if (!root) continue
    const abs = absOf(root, unit.path)
    if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) continue
    const parsed = parseProblemMarkdown(fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8"))
    return String((parsed.problem && parsed.problem.solution) || "").trim()
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * 一个单元还欠什么。
 * @param {object} unit
 * @param {{learningRoot?: string|null, contentRoots?: string[], warnings?: string[]}} opts
 * @returns {string[]} 缺口种类（契约 §8.4 的取值域）
 */
function missingOf(unit, opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const warnings = Array.isArray(o.warnings) ? o.warnings : []
  const missing = []
  const kind = String(unit.kind || "")
  const statement = String(unit.statement || "").trim()
  if (kind === "card") {
    if (!hasAssertScript(unit, o.learningRoot || null)) missing.push("asserts")
    if (statement === "" || statement === EMPTY_EXPLAIN) missing.push("statement")
    return missing
  }
  if (statement === "") missing.push("statement")
  if (!Array.isArray(unit.asserts) || unit.asserts.length === 0) missing.push("cases")
  const solution = readSolution(unit, o.contentRoots)
  if (solution === "") {
    missing.push("solution")
  } else if (solution === null) {
    const reference = String((unit.entry && unit.entry.reference) || "")
    if (reference === "") {
      warnings.push("读不到单元 " + String(unit.id) + " 的原文件，参考解法有没有欠账判断不了")
    }
  }
  return missing
}

/**
 * 扫描三轨的缺口。
 * @param {{units: object[], learningRoot?: string|null, contentRoots?: string[], track?: string|null}} opts
 * @returns {{tracks: object[], gaps: object[], warnings: string[]}}
 */
function scanGaps(opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const units = Array.isArray(o.units) ? o.units : []
  const learningRoot = o.learningRoot || null
  const contentRoots = Array.isArray(o.contentRoots) ? o.contentRoots : []
  const only = o.track ? String(o.track) : ""
  const warnings = []
  const gaps = []
  const counts = {}

  function bucket(code) {
    if (!counts[code]) {
      counts[code] = {
        track: code,
        total: 0,
        todo: 0,
        draft: 0,
        verified: 0,
        rejected: 0,
        reviewed: 0,
      }
    }
    return counts[code]
  }
  TRACK_CODES.forEach(bucket)

  units.forEach(function (unit) {
    const code = String(unit.track || "")
    if (!counts[code]) {
      bucket(code)
      warnings.push("出现了未知轨道：" + code)
    }
    const box = bucket(code)
    box.total += 1
    const status = String(unit.status || "todo")
    if (status === "todo" || status === "draft" || status === "verified" || status === "rejected") {
      box[status] += 1
    } else {
      box.todo += 1
      warnings.push("单元 " + String(unit.id) + " 的 status 不认识：" + status + "，按 todo 算")
    }
    if (unit.reviewed === true) box.reviewed += 1
    if (only && code !== only) return
    const missing = missingOf(unit, {
      learningRoot: learningRoot,
      contentRoots: contentRoots,
      warnings: warnings,
    })
    if (missing.length > 0) {
      gaps.push({
        id: String(unit.id),
        track: code,
        kind: String(unit.kind || ""),
        title: String(unit.title || ""),
        missing: missing,
      })
    }
  })

  const tracks = TRACK_CODES.filter(function (code) {
    return !only || code === only
  }).map(function (code) {
    return counts[code]
  })

  gaps.sort(function (a, b) {
    return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0
  })

  return { tracks: tracks, gaps: gaps, warnings: warnings }
}

/**
 * 把缺口清单写进 learning/units/<track>/plan.json（契约 §10 的 plan 子命令）。
 * @param {{result: object, unitsDir: string, repoRoot: string|null, stateFile: string|null}} opts
 * @returns {{written: string[], blocked: string[], reason: string}}
 */
function writePlanFiles(opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const result = o.result || { tracks: [], gaps: [] }
  const unitsDir = o.unitsDir || ""
  const written = []
  const blocked = []
  let reason = ""
  if (unitsDir === "") {
    return { written: written, blocked: blocked, reason: "还没连上 learning 仓，plan.json 写不了" }
  }
  const tracks = Array.isArray(result.tracks) ? result.tracks : []
  tracks.forEach(function (track) {
    const code = String(track.track || "")
    const dir = path.join(unitsDir, code)
    const file = path.join(dir, "plan.json")
    const gaps = (result.gaps || []).filter(function (gap) {
      return String(gap.track) === code
    })
    const payload = {
      track: code,
      generated_at: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      total: track.total,
      todo: track.todo,
      draft: track.draft,
      verified: track.verified,
      rejected: track.rejected,
      reviewed: track.reviewed,
      gaps: gaps,
    }
    const content = JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) + "\n"
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true })
    if (o.repoRoot) {
      const { writeFileChecked } = require("./unit-file.js")
      const res = writeFileChecked(file, content, {
        repoRoot: o.repoRoot,
        stateFile: o.stateFile || null,
      })
      if (!res.ok) {
        blocked.push(code)
        if (!reason) reason = res.reason
        return
      }
    } else {
      fs.writeFileSync(file, content, "utf8")
    }
    written.push(code)
  })
  return { written: written, blocked: blocked, reason: reason }
}

module.exports = {
  GAP_KINDS: GAP_KINDS,
  EMPTY_EXPLAIN: EMPTY_EXPLAIN,
  missingOf: missingOf,
  scanGaps: scanGaps,
  writePlanFiles: writePlanFiles,
}
```

### Task 7: 执行器反向校验 + 变异测试（`core/supply/verify.js`）

**Files:**
- 新建 `core/supply/verify.js`
- 新建 `tests/supply-verify.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 契约 §10 的 `verify` 判定规则表与**三种变异手法原文**、§1.4（loader B 只读，裁决写 loader C）、§1.5（写 `learning/units/<track>/<stem>.md`）、§3.5（断言脚本统一模板）、§3.4（loader C 优先于 loader B）、§7.3（`copyIntoSandbox`）、G18（沙箱）、G19（超时与输出上限）
- Produces:
  - `SUPPLY_STATUSES : string[]` —— `["verified", "rejected", "skipped"]`
  - `MUTATION_TECHNIQUES : string[]` —— `["assert", "if", "return"]`
  - `TECHNIQUE_LABELS : object` —— 三种手法的中文名
  - `DEFAULT_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS : number` —— `20000`
  - `mutateSource(source, technique) : {ok, code, technique, reason}`
  - `verifyUnit(params) : Promise<{id, status, reason, mutated, technique, stdout, stderr, exitCode, durationMs, wrote, diff}>`
  - `verifyBatch(params) : Promise<{results, counts, diffs}>`

**变异手法的实现口径**（逐条对应契约 §10，顺序不变）：

| 手法 | 契约原文 | 本计划的实现 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 把 demo 里第一条 `assert ` 语句改成 `assert False` | 扫描源码，第一条**行首（含缩进）为 `assert ` 的语句**改成同缩进的 `assert False`（行尾注释保留） |
| 2 | 把 demo 里第一条 `if ...:` 的条件改成 `if False:` | 扫描源码，第一条**块级 `if ...:`**（行尾是冒号）改成 `if False:`；**块级 `if` 一条都没有时**，退到**内联条件式** `A if 条件 else B` → `A if False else B` |
| 3 | 把 demo 里第一条 `return ` 语句改成 `return None` | 扫描源码，第一条**返回值不是 `None`** 的 `return` 改成 `return None`（行尾注释保留） |

两条**必须写下来的例外**（不违反契约，是把契约的规则落到实处时必须补的）：

- **入口守卫 `if __name__ == "__main__":` 不算手法 2 的可改点。** 它不是「被测逻辑的分支」，把它改成 `if False:` 会让整个程序什么都不做——那样即使断言脚本毫无内容也会「失败」，等于用「程序没跑」冒充「断言抓到了错误」，证明不了任何事。跳过它之后如果确实没有别的 `if`，手法 2 判为**不可用**，继续试手法 3。
- **手法 2 的内联条件式是补充，不是替换。** 实测（2026-11-12）16 张数据库卡里，`transaction` 卡**没有任何块级 `if`**，唯一的条件是一处 f-string 里的内联条件式（`'[OK] 相同（可重复读）' if bal1 == bal2 else '[NO] 不同（不可重复读！）'`）。spec §6.2 说的是「改一个条件 / 删一个分支」，内联条件式正是「改一个条件」，所以纳入手法 2。

**判定规则（逐条对应契约 §10 的 `verify` 判定表）**：

| 情况 | 结论 | 理由原文 |
|---|---|---|
| `kind !== "card"`（算法题） | `skipped` | 算法题由 P5 校验 |
| 断言脚本不存在 | `skipped` | 断言脚本还不存在：`<相对路径>` |
| demo 不存在 | `skipped` | 这个单元没有可运行的 demo |
| demo `import numpy` 而本机没装 numpy | `skipped` | 环境缺失：本机没有装 numpy，这张卡先跳过（不算通过也不算打回） |
| 断言脚本退出码非 0 | `rejected` | 断言脚本没有通过：`<输出摘要>` |
| 断言脚本退出码 0，但**找不到任何可改点** | `rejected` | 找不到可变异点，无法验证断言有效性 |
| 断言脚本退出码 0，改坏之后**脚本仍然退出 0** | `rejected` | 变异测试未捕获，断言无效 |
| 断言脚本退出码 0，改坏之后**脚本失败** | `verified` | 断言脚本通过，且变异测试证明断言有效（`<手法中文名>`） |

> **为什么「先改坏、再跑一次」就够**：`exit 0` 只证明「没崩」，一个 `assert True` 也能过；变异测试才证明断言真的在盯着被测逻辑。改坏之后脚本必须失败——这才是「断言有效」的证据。

**沙箱布局（G18）**：

```
<os.tmpdir()>/study-hub-judge-XXXX/       ← judge/sandbox.js 的 createSandbox() 建出来的
├── main.py                             ← demo 的沙箱副本（copyIntoSandbox）
└── demo.assert.py                      ← 断言脚本的沙箱副本（copyIntoSandbox）
```

> （临时目录前缀在 P0 已从 `algo-plan-judge-` 改名为 `study-hub-judge-`，见 P0 计划 Task 1。）

- 跑脚本时设 **`STUDY_HUB_DEMO=<沙箱>/main.py`**（契约 §3.5 的统一模板靠它找 demo；模板的缺省值「与脚本同目录的 `main.py`」在沙箱里也对得上）。
- `cwd` = 沙箱目录，demo 的 `__file__` 就在沙箱里 → demo 在自己旁边写的 `.db` 文件落在沙箱里，**learning 仓工作树一个文件都不会多**。
- **变异只改沙箱里的 `main.py` 副本**，learning 仓里的 `main.py` 一个字节都不动。
- 判完 `sandbox.cleanup()`（G18「判完即删」）。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败测试**

新建 `tests/supply-verify.test.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply-verify.test.js —— 执行器反向校验 + 变异测试
 *
 * 真实跑 Python（与判题器同一套执行器与沙箱），
 * 但**不联网**：断言脚本用 subprocess 跑本地的 demo。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")
const { execFileSync } = require("node:child_process")

const {
  verifyUnit,
  verifyBatch,
  mutateSource,
  MUTATION_TECHNIQUES,
  SUPPLY_STATUSES,
} = require("../core/supply/verify.js")
const { resolveToolchain } = require("../judge/sandbox.js")
const { stateFilePath } = require("../core/supply/unit-file.js")

const TOOLCHAIN = resolveToolchain({})
const HAS_PYTHON = Boolean(TOOLCHAIN.python)
const SKIP = HAS_PYTHON ? false : "本机没有可用的 Python 解释器，跑不了真实执行"

/** 契约 §3.5 冻结的断言脚本模板（这里按同样的形状写死一份） */
const SCRIPT_TEXT = [
  "#!/usr/bin/env python3",
  '"""demo 卡断言脚本 —— 校验 demo 的关键结论都出现在输出里。"""',
  "",
  "import os",
  "import shutil",
  "import subprocess",
  "import sys",
  "import tempfile",
  "",
  'DEMO = os.environ.get("STUDY_HUB_DEMO") or os.path.join(',
  '    os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "main.py"',
  ")",
  "",
  "CHECKS = [",
  '    ("算对了", "[[OK]] 算对了"),',
  "]",
  "",
  "",
  "def decode(raw):",
  '    """把字节流解成文本：utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 依次试。"""',
  '    for enc in ("utf-8", "gbk"):',
  "        try:",
  "            return raw.decode(enc)",
  "        except UnicodeDecodeError:",
  "            continue",
  '    return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")',
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  "    if not os.path.exists(DEMO):",
  '        print("找不到 demo：" + DEMO)',
  "        return 1",
  "    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='study-hub-assert-')",
  "    try:",
  "        target = os.path.join(sandbox, os.path.basename(DEMO))",
  "        shutil.copy2(DEMO, target)",
  "        proc = subprocess.run(",
  "            [sys.executable, target],",
  "            capture_output=True,",
  "            timeout=120,",
  "        )",
  "    finally:",
  "        shutil.rmtree(sandbox, ignore_errors=True)",
  "    out = decode(proc.stdout)",
  "    failed = []",
  "    for name, needle in CHECKS:",
  "        if needle not in out:",
  '            failed.append(name + "（缺少：" + needle + "）")',
  "    if proc.returncode != 0:",
  '        failed.append("demo 退出码非 0：" + str(proc.returncode))',
  "    if failed:",
  "        for item in failed:",
  '            print("断言失败：" + item)',
  "        return 1",
  '    print("全部断言通过（%d 条）" % len(CHECKS))',
  "    return 0",
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    sys.exit(main())",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 有分支、有返回值：变异改坏后输出会变，断言抓得住 */
const DEMO_MUTABLE = [
  "def compute(x):",
  "    return x + 1",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  "    value = compute(1)",
  "    if value == 2:",
  '        print("[[OK]] 算对了")',
  "    else:",
  '        print("[[FAIL]] 算错了")',
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    main()",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 恒定输出：改坏了也看不出来 → 断言无效 */
const DEMO_VACUOUS = [
  "def compute(x):",
  "    return x + 1",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  '    print("[[OK]] 算对了")',
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    main()",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 没有任何可改点 */
const DEMO_NO_POINT = ['print("[[OK]] 算对了")', ""].join("\n")

/** demo 自己就崩 */
const DEMO_BROKEN = ['raise RuntimeError("坏了")', ""].join("\n")

/** 依赖 numpy */
const DEMO_NUMPY = [
  "import numpy as np",
  "",
  'print("[[OK]] 算对了")',
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 造一个假的 learning 仓（真的 git 仓，好查脏） */
function makeRepo(demoText, withScript) {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-verify-repo-"))
  execFileSync("git", ["init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.email", "t@t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.name", "t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const cardDir = path.join(root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo")
  fs.mkdirSync(cardDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "main.py"), demoText, "utf8")
  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units")
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database"), { recursive: true })
  if (withScript !== false) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.assert.py"), SCRIPT_TEXT, "utf8")
  }
  execFileSync("git", ["add", "-A"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["commit", "-m", "init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const stateDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-verify-state-"))
  return { root: root, unitsDir: unitsDir, stateFile: stateFilePath(stateDir) }
}

/** 一张卡的单元 */
function cardUnit(patch) {
  const p = patch || {}
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: "db:demo",
      track: "database",
      kind: "card",
      title: "演示卡",
      statement: "# 演示卡\n\n要点\n",
      entry: {
        demo: "scaffold/grokking/demo/main.py",
        reference: "",
        graded: "script",
        script: "units/database/demo.assert.py",
        language: "python",
      },
      asserts: [],
      topics: [],
      difficulty: "medium",
      source: "grokking",
      status: "todo",
      progress: "todo",
      reviewed: false,
      reviewed_at: "",
      subtracks: [],
      path: "scaffold/grokking/demo/",
    },
    p
  )
}

/** 跑一次校验 */
function run(repo, unit, extra) {
  const o = extra || {}
  return verifyUnit(
    Object.assign(
      {
        unit: unit,
        learningRoot: repo.root,
        unitsDir: repo.unitsDir,
        repoRoot: repo.root,
        stateFile: repo.stateFile,
        numpyAvailable: true,
      },
      o
    )
  )
}

test("供给结论只有三种，且与 verdict 五类不是一回事", function () {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(SUPPLY_STATUSES, ["verified", "rejected", "skipped"])
  assert.deepStrictEqual(MUTATION_TECHNIQUES, ["assert", "if", "return"])
})

test("mutateSource：手法 1 把第一条 assert 改成 assert False", function () {
  const src = ["def f():", "    assert x == 1", "    assert y == 2", ""].join("\n")
  const m = mutateSource(src, "assert")
  assert.strictEqual(m.ok, true)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("assert False") !== -1, m.code)
  assert.strictEqual(m.code.indexOf("assert x == 1"), -1)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("assert y == 2") !== -1, "只改第一条")
})

test("mutateSource：手法 2 改块级 if，但不碰入口守卫", function () {
  const src = [
    'if __name__ == "__main__":',
    "    main()",
    "",
    "def f(x):",
    "    if x > 0:",
    "        return 1",
    "    return 0",
    "",
  ].join("\n")
  const m = mutateSource(src, "if")
  assert.strictEqual(m.ok, true)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("if False:") !== -1, m.code)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf('if __name__ == "__main__":') !== -1, "入口守卫不许动")
})

test("mutateSource：手法 2 在没有块级 if 时改内联条件式", function () {
  const src = [
    "def f(a, b):",
    '    return f"{a} if a == b else 别的"',
    "",
  ].join("\n")
  const m = mutateSource(src, "if")
  assert.strictEqual(m.ok, true)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("if False else") !== -1, m.code)
})

test("mutateSource：手法 3 把第一条 return 改成 return None", function () {
  const src = ["def f(x):", "    return x + 1", "", "def g(y):", "    return y", ""].join("\n")
  const m = mutateSource(src, "return")
  assert.strictEqual(m.ok, true)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("return None") !== -1, m.code)
  assert.ok(m.code.indexOf("return y") !== -1, "只改第一条")
})

test("mutateSource：找不到可改点时 ok=false 且给中文理由", function () {
  const m = mutateSource('print("hi")\n', "assert")
  assert.strictEqual(m.ok, false)
  assert.ok(m.reason.indexOf("assert") !== -1, m.reason)
})

test("断言通过 + 变异被捕获 → verified，并把 loader C 单元文件写成 verified", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE, true)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "verified", result.reason)
  assert.strictEqual(result.mutated, true)
  assert.strictEqual(result.technique, "if")
  const file = path.join(repo.unitsDir, "database", "demo.md")
  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(file), "单元文件要被写出来")
  const text = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("status: verified") !== -1, text)
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("id: db:demo") !== -1, text)
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("script: units/database/demo.assert.py") !== -1, text)
})

test("断言脚本没通过 → rejected", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_BROKEN, true)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "rejected", result.reason)
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("断言脚本没有通过") !== -1, result.reason)
})

test("断言恒真（改坏了也看不出来）→ rejected：变异测试未捕获，断言无效", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_VACUOUS, true)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "rejected", result.reason)
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("变异测试未捕获，断言无效") !== -1, result.reason)
})

test("找不到可变异点 → rejected", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_NO_POINT, true)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "rejected", result.reason)
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("找不到可变异点，无法验证断言有效性") !== -1, result.reason)
})

test("缺 numpy → skipped，不算通过也不算打回", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_NUMPY, true)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit(), { numpyAvailable: false })
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "skipped", result.reason)
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("环境缺失") !== -1, result.reason)
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("numpy") !== -1, result.reason)
})

test("算法题 → skipped，理由是「算法题由 P5 校验」", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE, true)
  const unit = cardUnit({ id: "algo:lc-00007", track: "algorithm", kind: "problem" })
  const result = await run(repo, unit)

  assert.strictEqual(result.reason, "算法题由 P5 校验")
})

test("断言脚本不存在 → skipped", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE, false)
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "skipped")
  assert.ok(result.reason.indexOf("断言脚本还不存在") !== -1, result.reason)
})

test("跑完之后 learning 仓里不多出任何文件，demo 原文也没被动过", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE, true)
  const before = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo.root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo", "main.py"), "utf8")
  const result = await run(repo, cardUnit())
  assert.strictEqual(result.status, "verified", result.reason)
  const after = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo.root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo", "main.py"), "utf8")
  assert.strictEqual(after, before, "learning 仓里的 demo 一个字节都不许动")
  const strays = execFileSync(
    "git",
    ["-c", "core.quotepath=false", "status", "--porcelain", "-z", "--untracked-files=all"],
    { cwd: repo.root, encoding: "utf8" }
  )
  const paths = strays.split("\0").filter(function (s) { return s !== "" })
  paths.forEach(function (p) {
    assert.strictEqual(/\.db$/i.test(p), false, "learning 仓里不许多出 .db 文件：" + p)
  })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(paths, ["?? units/database/demo.md"], "只该多出单元文件：" + paths.join("、"))
})

test("verifyBatch 汇总三种结论的计数", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE, true)
  const batch = await verifyBatch({
    units: [
      cardUnit({ id: "db:a" }),
      cardUnit({ id: "db:b", kind: "problem" }),
      cardUnit({ id: "db:c", entry: { demo: "scaffold/grokking/demo/main.py", reference: "", graded: "script", script: "units/database/nope.assert.py", language: "python" } }),
    ],
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    unitsDir: repo.unitsDir,
    repoRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
    numpyAvailable: true,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(batch.counts.total, 3)
  assert.strictEqual(batch.counts.verified, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(batch.counts.skipped, 2)
  assert.strictEqual(batch.counts.rejected, 0)
})
```

```bash
cd projects/apps/study-hub && node tests/supply-verify.test.js
```

**Expected（失败）**：

```
Error: Cannot find module '../core/supply/verify.js'
```

- [ ] **Step 3：写最小实现**

新建 `core/supply/verify.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * verify.js —— 执行器反向校验 + 变异测试（契约 §10）
 *
 * 一句话：**不用相信 LLM，用执行器验它。**
 *
 * 卡类单元（kind=card）的校验动作：
 *   1. 把 demo 与断言脚本都复制进沙箱（judge/sandbox.js，G18）；
 *   2. 真跑一遍断言脚本 —— 必须 exit 0；
 *   3. **变异测试**：把沙箱里的 demo 副本改坏，重跑断言脚本 —— 它**必须失败**。
 *      exit 0 只证明「没崩」，一个 assert True 也能过；变异测试才证明断言真的在盯着被测逻辑。
 *   4. 结论写进 loader C 的单元文件（learning/units/<track>/<stem>.md）：
 *      draft → verified 或 draft → rejected。loader B 自己只读，永远不写盘（契约 §1.5）。
 *
 * 结论词是「供给结论」，不是 judge 的 verdict——verdict 五类（pass/wrong_answer/…）冻结不变（G20）。
 */

const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")

const { createSandbox, runCommand, resolveToolchain, copyIntoSandbox } = require("../../judge/sandbox.js")
const {
  renderUnitFile,
  patchUnitFile,
  writeFileChecked,
  unitFilePath,
  stemOf,
} = require("./unit-file.js")

/** 供给结论（不是 verdict；verdict 五类冻结，G20） */
const SUPPLY_STATUSES = ["verified", "rejected", "skipped"]

/** 三种变异手法（契约 §10 的原文顺序） */
const MUTATION_TECHNIQUES = ["assert", "if", "return"]

/** 手法的中文名 */
const TECHNIQUE_LABELS = {
  assert: "把第一条 assert 改成 assert False",
  if: "把第一条 if 的条件改成 if False",
  return: "把第一条 return 改成 return None",
}

/** 供给侧跑的是整个 demo（不是判题器的单个用例），时限比 G19 的 2000ms 宽 */
const DEFAULT_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS = 20000

/**
 * 把一行拆成「代码部分 + 行尾注释」。
 * @param {string} text
 * @returns {{code: string, comment: string}}
 */
function splitComment(text) {
  const at = String(text).indexOf("#")
  if (at === -1) return { code: String(text), comment: "" }
  return { code: String(text).slice(0, at), comment: String(text).slice(at).trim() }
}

/**
 * 变异一份 demo 源码。**只对沙箱副本调用**，绝不动 learning 仓里的文件（G18）。
 * @param {string} source
 * @param {string} technique "assert" | "if" | "return"
 * @returns {{ok: boolean, code: string, technique: string, reason: string}}
 */
function mutateSource(source, technique) {
  const src = String(source === null || source === undefined ? "" : source)
  const eol = src.indexOf("\r\n") !== -1 ? "\r\n" : "\n"
  const lines = src.split(/\r\n|\n/)

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i]

    if (technique === "assert") {
      const m = /^(\s*)assert\s+(.*)$/.exec(line)
      if (!m) continue
      const parts = splitComment(m[2])
      if (parts.code.trim() === "") continue
      lines[i] = m[1] + "assert False" + (parts.comment ? " " + parts.comment : "")
      return { ok: true, code: lines.join(eol), technique: technique, reason: "" }
    }

    if (technique === "if") {
      // 2a. 块级 if ...:
      const m = /^(\s*)if\s+(.*)$/.exec(line)
      if (m) {
        const raw = m[2].trim()
        if (raw.endsWith(":")) {
          const parts = splitComment(raw.slice(0, -1))
          const cond = parts.code.trim()
          const isMainGuard =
            cond === '__name__ == "__main__"' || cond === "__name__ == '__main__'"
          if (cond !== "" && !isMainGuard) {
            lines[i] =
              m[1] + "if False:" + (parts.comment ? " " + parts.comment : "")
            return { ok: true, code: lines.join(eol), technique: technique, reason: "" }
          }
        }
        continue
      }
      // 2b. 内联条件式 A if 条件 else B
      const m2 = /^(\s*)(.*?)\s+if\s+(.+?)\s+else\s+(.*)$/.exec(line)
      if (m2) {
        const cond = m2[3].trim()
        if (cond !== "") {
          lines[i] =
            m2[1] +
            m2[2] +
            " if False else " +
            m2[4]
          return { ok: true, code: lines.join(eol), technique: technique, reason: "" }
        }
      }
      continue
    }

    if (technique === "return") {
      const m = /^(\s*)return\s+(.*)$/.exec(line)
      if (!m) continue
      const parts = splitComment(m[2])
      const value = parts.code.trim()
      if (value === "" || value === "None") continue
      lines[i] = m[1] + "return None" + (parts.comment ? " " + parts.comment : "")
      return { ok: true, code: lines.join(eol), technique: technique, reason: "" }
    }
  }

  const reasons = {
    assert: "demo 里没有 assert 语句",
    if: "demo 里没有可改的条件分支（入口守卫不算）",
    return: "demo 里没有可改的 return 语句",
  }
  return {
    ok: false,
    code: "",
    technique: technique,
    reason: reasons[technique] || "没有这个变异手法",
  }
}

/**
 * 探测本机有没有 numpy。
 * @param {{cmd: string, args: string[]}} python
 * @param {number} timeoutMs
 * @returns {Promise<boolean>}
 */
async function probeNumpy(python, timeoutMs) {
  const run = await runCommand(
    python.cmd,
    (python.args || []).concat(["-c", "import numpy"]),
    { cwd: os.tmpdir(), timeoutMs: timeoutMs }
  )
  return run.code === 0
}

/**
 * 把 stdout / stderr 摘成一句能给人看的理由。
 * @param {string} stdout
 * @param {string} stderr
 * @returns {string}
 */
function summarize(stdout, stderr) {
  const out = String(stdout || "").trim()
  const err = String(stderr || "").trim()
  const text = out !== "" ? out : err
  if (text === "") return "（没有输出）"
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(function (line) {
    return line.trim() !== ""
  })
  const picked = lines.slice(0, 3).join(" / ")
  return picked.length > 200 ? picked.slice(0, 200) + "…" : picked
}

/**
 * 把裁决写进 loader C 的单元文件（契约 §1.5）。
 * @param {object} unit
 * @param {string} status "verified" | "rejected"
 * @param {object} p verifyUnit 的入参
 * @returns {{ok: boolean, reason: string, diff: string}}
 */
function writeVerdict(unit, status, p) {
  const dir = p.unitsDir || null
  const root = p.repoRoot || p.learningRoot || null
  if (!dir || !root) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用", diff: "" }
  }
  const file = unitFilePath(dir, unit)
  const opts = { repoRoot: root, stateFile: p.stateFile || null }
  if (fs.existsSync(file)) {
    return patchUnitFile(file, { status: status }, opts)
  }
  const entry = unit.entry || {}
  const fields = {
    id: unit.id,
    track: unit.track,
    kind: "card",
    title: unit.title || "",
    difficulty: unit.difficulty || "medium",
    topics: Array.isArray(unit.topics) ? unit.topics : [],
    source: unit.source || "grokking",
    language: entry.language || "python",
    demo: entry.demo || "",
    reference: entry.reference || "",
    graded: "script",
    script: entry.script || "",
    status: status,
    reviewed: false,
    reviewed_at: "",
    subtracks: Array.isArray(unit.subtracks) ? unit.subtracks : [],
  }
  const content = renderUnitFile(fields, {
    explain: String(unit.statement || ""),
    related: [],
  })
  return writeFileChecked(file, content, opts)
}

/**
 * 校验一个单元：真跑一遍断言脚本，再做变异测试。
 * @param {{
 *   unit: object,
 *   learningRoot?: string|null,
 *   unitsDir?: string|null,
 *   repoRoot?: string|null,
 *   stateFile?: string|null,
 *   pythonOverride?: string|null,
 *   numpyAvailable?: boolean|null,
 *   timeoutMs?: number
 * }} params
 * @returns {Promise<{
 *   id: string, status: string, reason: string, mutated: boolean, technique: string,
 *   stdout: string, stderr: string, exitCode: number|null, durationMs: number,
 *   wrote: boolean, diff: string
 * }>}
 */
async function verifyUnit(params) {
  const p = params || {}
  const unit = p.unit || {}
  const timeoutMs = Number(p.timeoutMs) > 0 ? Number(p.timeoutMs) : DEFAULT_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS
  const result = {
    id: String(unit.id || ""),
    status: "skipped",
    reason: "",
    mutated: false,
    technique: "",
    stdout: "",
    stderr: "",
    exitCode: null,
    durationMs: 0,
    wrote: false,
    diff: "",
  }

  /** 记一个结论并（对 verified / rejected）写回单元文件 */
  function settle(status, reason) {
    result.status = status
    result.reason = reason
    if (status === "skipped") return result
    const written = writeVerdict(unit, status, p)
    result.wrote = written.ok
    result.diff = written.diff
    if (!written.ok) {
      result.reason = reason + "；但单元文件没写回：" + written.reason
    }
    return result
  }

  if (String(unit.kind || "") !== "card") {
    return settle("skipped", "算法题由 P5 校验")
  }
  const entry = unit.entry || {}
  const scriptRel = String(entry.script || "")
  const demoRel = String(entry.demo || "")
  if (scriptRel === "") return settle("skipped", "这个单元没有断言脚本路径")
  if (demoRel === "") return settle("skipped", "这个单元没有可运行的 demo")

  const root = p.learningRoot || p.repoRoot || null
  if (!root) return settle("skipped", "还没连上 learning 仓，校验不可用")

  const scriptAbs = path.join(root, scriptRel.split("/").join(path.sep))
  const demoAbs = path.join(root, demoRel.split("/").join(path.sep))
  if (!fs.existsSync(scriptAbs)) {
    return settle("skipped", "断言脚本还不存在：" + scriptRel)
  }
  if (!fs.existsSync(demoAbs)) {
    return settle("skipped", "demo 源码还不存在：" + demoRel)
  }

  const toolchain = resolveToolchain({ pythonOverride: p.pythonOverride || null })
  const python = toolchain.python
  if (!python) {
    return settle("skipped", "本机找不到可用的 Python 解释器，这个单元先跳过")
  }

  const demoSource = fs.readFileSync(demoAbs, "utf8")
  const needsNumpy = /^\s*(import\s+numpy|from\s+numpy\s+import)/m.test(demoSource)
  if (needsNumpy) {
    const hasNumpy =
      p.numpyAvailable === undefined || p.numpyAvailable === null
        ? await probeNumpy(python, timeoutMs)
        : Boolean(p.numpyAvailable)
    if (!hasNumpy) {
      return settle(
        "skipped",
        "环境缺失：本机没有装 numpy，这张卡先跳过（不算通过也不算打回）"
      )
    }
  }

  const sandbox = createSandbox()
  try {
    const demoInSandbox = copyIntoSandbox(sandbox, demoAbs, "main.py")
    const scriptInSandbox = copyIntoSandbox(
      sandbox,
      scriptAbs,
      stemOf(unit) + ".assert.py"
    )
    const env = Object.assign({}, process.env, { STUDY_HUB_DEMO: demoInSandbox })
    const args = (python.args || []).concat([scriptInSandbox])

    const first = await runCommand(python.cmd, args, {
      cwd: sandbox.dir,
      env: env,
      timeoutMs: timeoutMs,
    })
    result.stdout = first.stdout
    result.stderr = first.stderr
    result.exitCode = first.code
    result.durationMs = first.durationMs

    if (first.timedOut) {
      return settle("rejected", "断言脚本超时（超过 " + timeoutMs + " 毫秒）")
    }
    if (first.code !== 0) {
      return settle("rejected", "断言脚本没有通过：" + summarize(first.stdout, first.stderr))
    }

    let mutant = null
    for (const technique of MUTATION_TECHNIQUES) {
      const m = mutateSource(demoSource, technique)
      if (m.ok) {
        mutant = m
        break
      }
    }
    if (!mutant) {
      return settle("rejected", "找不到可变异点，无法验证断言有效性")
    }
    result.mutated = true
    result.technique = mutant.technique

    // 只改沙箱里的副本，learning 仓里的 demo 一个字节都不动（G18）
    fs.writeFileSync(demoInSandbox, mutant.code, "utf8")
    const second = await runCommand(python.cmd, args, {
      cwd: sandbox.dir,
      env: env,
      timeoutMs: timeoutMs,
    })
    if (second.timedOut) {
      return settle("rejected", "变异测试未捕获，断言无效（变异后脚本超时）")
    }
    if (second.code === 0) {
      return settle("rejected", "变异测试未捕获，断言无效")
    }
    return settle(
      "verified",
      "断言脚本通过，且变异测试证明断言有效（" + TECHNIQUE_LABELS[mutant.technique] + "）"
    )
  } finally {
    sandbox.cleanup()
  }
}

/**
 * 批量校验。
 * @param {{units: object[], learningRoot?: string|null, unitsDir?: string|null,
 *          repoRoot?: string|null, stateFile?: string|null,
 *          numpyAvailable?: boolean|null, timeoutMs?: number}} params
 * @returns {Promise<{results: object[], counts: {total, verified, rejected, skipped}, diffs: string[]}>}
 */
async function verifyBatch(params) {
  const p = params || {}
  const units = Array.isArray(p.units) ? p.units : []
  const results = []
  for (const unit of units) {
    results.push(await verifyUnit(Object.assign({}, p, { unit: unit })))
  }
  const counts = { total: results.length, verified: 0, rejected: 0, skipped: 0 }
  results.forEach(function (item) {
    if (item.status === "verified") counts.verified += 1
    else if (item.status === "rejected") counts.rejected += 1
    else counts.skipped += 1
  })
  const diffs = results
    .map(function (item) {
      return item.diff
    })
    .filter(function (diff) {
      return diff !== ""
    })
  return { results: results, counts: counts, diffs: diffs }
}

module.exports = {
  SUPPLY_STATUSES: SUPPLY_STATUSES,
  MUTATION_TECHNIQUES: MUTATION_TECHNIQUES,
  TECHNIQUE_LABELS: TECHNIQUE_LABELS,
  DEFAULT_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS: DEFAULT_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS,
  mutateSource: mutateSource,
  probeNumpy: probeNumpy,
  summarize: summarize,
  writeVerdict: writeVerdict,
  verifyUnit: verifyUnit,
  verifyBatch: verifyBatch,
}
```

**Files:**

- 新建 `tools/supply.js`
- 新建 `tests/supply-cli.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes（上游签名逐条对应，括号里是产出它的那一期/那个 Task）：
  - `core/paths.js`（**P0 交付**，G16）：`resolveLearningRoot(env, override) : {root: string|null, ok: boolean, reason: string}`、`localBankDir(env, override) : string` —— learning 仓路径**一律经它解析**，不硬编码
  - `core/track/tracks.js`（**P0 交付**，契约 §14）：`TRACK_CODES : string[]`、`labelOf(code) : string`
  - `core/unit/index.js`（**P0 交付**，契约 §3.4）：`loadUnits({learningRoot, localBank, unitsDir, grokkingDir}) : {units: Unit[], warnings: string[], byId: object}`
  - `core/llm/client.js`（**Task 1 产出**）：`createLlmClient(opts)` —— 缺 provider / 缺 key / 关掉开关时返回 `NullClient`（`name === "null"`）
  - `core/llm/service.js`（**Task 5 产出**）：`createLlmService(opts)`、`readLlmSettings(store)`；服务方法 `generateAssert(req) : Promise<{unit, script, topics, model, file, unitFile}>`、`writeExplain(req) : Promise<{unit, explain, model, file}>`、`currentClient()`
  - `core/supply/plan.js`（**Task 6 产出**）：`scanGaps(opts) : {tracks, gaps, warnings}`、`writePlanFiles(opts) : {written: string[], blocked: string[], reason: string}`、`missingOf(unit, opts) : string[]`、`GAP_KINDS : string[]`
  - `core/supply/verify.js`（**Task 7 产出**）：`verifyBatch(params) : Promise<{results, counts: {total, verified, rejected, skipped}, diffs: string[]}>`
  - `core/supply/unit-file.js`（**Task 4 产出**）：`unitFilePath(unitsDir, unit) : string`、`applyFrontmatterPatch(text, patch) : string`、`appendSectionNote(text, name, note) : string`、`writeFileChecked(absPath, content, opts) : {ok, changed, reason, diff}`、`stateFilePath(appRoot) : string`
  - `core/sync/rounds.js`（**P0 交付**）：`assertCleanWorktree(repoRoot)`、`makeDiff(file, before, after)`、`splitLines`（**Step 1 补上导出**）
  - `server/db.js`（**P0 交付**）：`openDb(file)`、`createStore(db)`
  - `core/bank/provider.js`（**P0 交付**）：`FileProvider(bankDir)`
- Produces:
  - `core/supply/review.js`：
    - `REVIEW_SECTION : string` —— `"关联"`（抽检意见追加到这里的小节名）
    - `todayStamp(date?) : string` —— `YYYY-MM-DD`
    - `pickReviewTargets(units, opts) : object[]` —— 待抽检单元（已验证优先，其次待校验，最后待补内容；同级按 id 升序）
    - `applyReview(opts) : {ok, code, changed, reason, diff, unit}` —— `code` 取值 `"ok" | "blocked" | "missing" | "invalid"`；`unit` 为 `{id, status, reviewed, reviewed_at}` 或 `null`
  - `tools/supply.js`：
    - `COMMANDS : string[]` —— `["plan", "generate", "verify", "review", "report"]`
    - `EXIT_OK : number` = `0`、`EXIT_USAGE : number` = `1`、`EXIT_LLM_OFF : number` = `2`
    - `parseArgs(argv) : {command, options, error}`
    - `runCli(argv, ctx) : Promise<{code, stdout, stderr}>`
    - `main(argv) : Promise<number>`

**CLI 契约（逐条对应契约 §10）**：

| 子命令 | 选项 | 行为 | 退出码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `plan` | `--track <t>` `--limit <n>` | 扫描缺口清单，打到 stdout，并写 `learning/units/<track>/plan.json` | 0 |
| `generate` | `--track <t>` `--limit <n>` `--session <batchId>` | 调 LLM 产出断言脚本 + 讲解正文，落 `learning/units/<track>/`，`status: draft` | 0；LLM 关掉 → **2**；参数错 / learning 仓路径解析不出来 → **1** |
| `verify` | `--all` \| `--track <t>` \| `--unit <id>` | 跑执行器反向校验：`draft → verified` 或 `draft → rejected` | 0（有不过项也退出 0，**结果打在输出里**） |
| `review` | `--sample <n>` `--track <t>` | 抽检：打印待抽检单元（含题面与断言），人点通过 / 打回 → 写回 frontmatter | 0 |
| `report` | `--track <t>` | 打印每轨的 `todo/draft/verified/rejected` 计数与欠账 | 0 |

**退出码**（契约 §10.1 + G10）：

- `0` = 正常跑完（**包括有单元被打回**——结果打在输出里，不靠退出码表达）。
- `1` = 参数错 / learning 仓路径解析不出来（契约 §10.1 原文：「`1` = 参数错 / learning 仓路径解析不出来」）。
- `2` = LLM 关掉（契约 §10 原文：「0（LLM 关掉时 503 语义 → 退出码 2 + 中文提示）」）。

**LLM 关掉时的口径（G10 + G30）**：**只有 `generate` 受影响**（退出码 2 + 中文提示）；`plan` / `verify` / `review` / `report` **都不调 LLM，照常可用**（退出码 0）。
> **口径说明（必须写下来，免得做到一半改口径）**：G30 那行前半句写着「LLM 关掉时只有 `plan` 与 `report` 可用」，后半句的括号却写着「`verify` / `review` 不调 LLM，照常可用」——**前半句与括号自相矛盾**。G10 写的是「`plan` / `verify` / `review` / `report` 与 `GET /api/supply/status` **完全不受影响**」，契约 §12 的 P4 验收线写的是「LLM 关掉时 `plan` / `verify` / `report` 照常可用」。**以 G10 + 契约 §12 为准**：只有 `generate` 受 LLM 开关影响。

**纯本地模式（G17）**：解析不到 learning 仓时——

- `plan` / `verify` / `report`：**打印中文提示并正常退出（0）**，只处理本地题库里的单元。
- `generate` / `review`：要往 learning 仓写回，**退出码 1**，提示「还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用」。

**密钥纪律（G9）**：本 CLI **不接受** `--api-key` / `--token` / `--secret` 这类参数；解析到就直接报错退出（退出码 1）。密钥只从环境变量 / `.env` 读。

**不自动提交（G13）**：CLI 写回 learning 仓后**只打印 diff**，**绝不**执行 `git commit` / `git push`。

**抽检候选的口径**：待抽检 = `unit.reviewed !== true` 的单元；排序上「已验证」优先，其次「待校验」，最后「待补内容」，同级按 id 升序；`--sample <n>` 取前 n 个。

```js
/** 契约 §3.5 冻结的断言脚本模板（新版：subprocess + 临时目录 + 不写死 text=True）——
 *  与契约里的模板**逐字一致**，只把 <stem> 换成 demo、CHECKS 里的一条换成
 *  「算对了」。三条硬性约定都在里面：
 *    ① demo 用 subprocess 跑，不 import；
 *    ② 脚本自己把 demo 复制进 tempfile.mkdtemp() 造的临时目录再跑，跑完 rmtree；
 *    ③ 不写死 text=True——抓字节，再按 utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 依次试解码。 */
const SCRIPT_TEXT = [
  "#!/usr/bin/env python3",
  '"""demo 卡断言脚本 —— 校验 demo 的关键结论都出现在输出里。',

  "  2) 指定 demo：环境变量 STUDY_HUB_DEMO 指向 demo 的绝对路径。",
  "全部断言通过 → exit 0；任一条不通过 → 打印失败原因并 exit 1。",
  "",
  "编码：demo 的 stdout 按**本机 locale** 编码（Windows 中文环境是 GBK，Linux 是 UTF-8），",
  "所以这里抓字节再按 utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 的顺序试解码，不写死编码。",
  '"""',
  "",
  "import os",
  "import shutil",
  "import subprocess",
  "import sys",
  "import tempfile",
  "",

  '    os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "main.py"',
  ")",
  "",
  "# (说明, 在 stdout 里必须出现的片段)",
  "CHECKS = [",
  '    ("算对了", "[[OK]] 算对了"),',
  "]",
  "",
  "",
  "def decode(raw):",
  '    """把字节流解成文本：utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 依次试。"""',
  '    for enc in ("utf-8", "gbk"):',
  "        try:",
  "            return raw.decode(enc)",
  "        except UnicodeDecodeError:",
  "            continue",
  '    return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")',
  "",
  "",
  "def run_demo():",
  '    """把 demo 复制进临时目录再跑。',
  "",
  "    为什么要复制：这几个 demo 会在 __file__ 旁边写 .db 文件，而且不能重复跑。",
  "    复制进临时目录，既保证每次都是干净环境，也保证 .db 不落进 learning 仓。",
  '    """',
  "    if not os.path.exists(DEMO):",
  '        return None, "找不到 demo：" + DEMO',
  '    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="study-hub-assert-")',
  "    try:",
  "        target = os.path.join(sandbox, os.path.basename(DEMO))",
  "        shutil.copy2(DEMO, target)",
  "        proc = subprocess.run(",
  "            [sys.executable, target],",
  "            capture_output=True,",
  "            timeout=180,",
  "        )",
  "        return proc, None",
  "    finally:",
  "        shutil.rmtree(sandbox, ignore_errors=True)",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  "    proc, err = run_demo()",
  "    if err is not None:",
  "        print(err)",
  "        return 1",
  "    out = decode(proc.stdout)",
  "    failed = []",
  "    for name, needle in CHECKS:",
  "        if needle not in out:",
  '            failed.append(name + "（缺少：" + needle + "）")',
  "    if proc.returncode != 0:",
  '        failed.append("demo 退出码非 0：" + str(proc.returncode))',
  '        failed.append("demo 的 stderr 末尾：" + decode(proc.stderr)[-500:])',
  "    if failed:",
  "        for item in failed:",
  '            print("断言失败：" + item)',
  "        return 1",
  '    print("全部断言通过（%d 条）" % len(CHECKS))',
  "    return 0",
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    sys.exit(main())",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 有分支、有返回值：变异改坏后输出会变，断言抓得住 */
const DEMO_MUTABLE = [

/** 造一个假的 learning 仓（真的 git 仓，好查脏） */
function makeRepo(opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-cli-repo-"))
  execFileSync("git", ["init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.email", "t@t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.name", "t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const cardDir = path.join(root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo")
  fs.mkdirSync(cardDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "main.py"), o.demo || DEMO_MUTABLE, "utf8")
  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units")
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database"), { recursive: true })
  if (o.withScript !== false) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.assert.py"), SCRIPT_TEXT, "utf8")
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), UNIT_FILE, "utf8")
  execFileSync("git", ["add", "-A"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["commit", "-m", "init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const stateDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-cli-state-"))
  return {
    root: root,
    unitsDir: unitsDir,
    stateFile: path.join(stateDir, "self-written.json"),
  }
}

/** 假 LLM 客户端：第一次回断言脚本，第二次回讲解正文 */
function fakeClient() {
  const calls = []
  let n = 0
  return {
    name: "fake",
    model: "fake-model",
    calls: calls,
    complete: async function (req) {
      calls.push(req)
      n += 1
      if (n === 1) return JSON.stringify({ assert_script: SCRIPT_TEXT, topics: ["事务"] })
      return "# 讲解\n\n这是假模型写的讲解正文。\n"
    },
  }
}

/** 假 ask：人一律点「通过」 */
function fakeAsk(answer) {
  const seen = []
  const fn = async function (unit) {
    seen.push(String(unit.id))
    return answer || { passed: true, note: "" }
  }
  fn.seen = seen
  return fn
}

/** 跑一次 CLI */
function cli(argv, ctx) {
  const base = {
    env: {},
    learningRoot: null,
    localBank: null,
    dbPath: ":memory:",
    stateFile: null,
    client: null,
    ask: null,
  }
  return runCli(argv, Object.assign(base, ctx || {}))
}

/** git 里有多少个提交（用来证明 CLI 没提交） */
function commitCount(root) {
  const out = execFileSync("git", ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"], {
    cwd: root,
    encoding: "utf8",
  })
  return Number(String(out).trim())
}

test("CLI 的子命令只有五个", function () {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(COMMANDS, ["plan", "generate", "verify", "review", "report"])
})

test("parseArgs：认得五个子命令", function () {
  assert.strictEqual(parseArgs(["plan"]).command, "plan")
  assert.strictEqual(parseArgs(["report", "--track", "database"]).command, "report")
  assert.strictEqual(parseArgs(["report", "--track", "database"]).options.track, "database")
  assert.strictEqual(parseArgs(["verify", "--all"]).options.all, true)
})

test("parseArgs：不接受把密钥写在命令行上（G9）", function () {
  const parsed = parseArgs(["generate", "--api-key", "sk-xxx"])
  assert.strictEqual(parsed.error !== "", true)
  assert.ok(parsed.error.indexOf("密钥") !== -1, parsed.error)
})

test("parseArgs：不认识的子命令 / 不该给这个子命令的选项都要报错", function () {
  assert.ok(parseArgs(["nope"]).error.indexOf("子命令") !== -1)
  assert.ok(parseArgs(["verify", "--sample", "5"]).error.indexOf("不接受选项") !== -1)
  assert.ok(parseArgs([]).error.indexOf("子命令") !== -1)
})
```
