- [ ] **Step 4：实现 `core/supply/review.js`（抽检写回，CLI 与接口共用同一份）**

新建 `core/supply/review.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * review.js —— 人工抽检的写回逻辑（CLI 的 review 子命令与 HTTP 接口共用同一份）
 *
 * 抽检 = 人看过一遍，给个结论。结论只有两种：
 *   - 通过（passed = true）→ frontmatter 记 reviewed: true / reviewed_at，status 不动
 *   - 打回（passed = false）→ status 改成 rejected，同样记 reviewed: true / reviewed_at
 *
 * 三条纪律：
 *   1. **写回只改目标行**（G15）：frontmatter 里只替换 reviewed / reviewed_at
 *      （打回时多一个 status）所在的那几行；抽检意见作为一条列表项追加到
 *      「## 关联」小节末尾；其余行逐字节不变（含 CRLF / LF）。
 *   2. **写回前查脏**（G14）：别人动过的文件一律阻塞，返回中文理由。
 *   3. **绝不自动提交**（G13）：本模块只产出 diff，不执行 git commit / git push。
 */

const fs = require("node:fs")

const {
  unitFilePath,
  applyFrontmatterPatch,
  appendSectionNote,
  writeFileChecked,
} = require("./unit-file.js")

/** 抽检意见追加到哪个小节 */
const REVIEW_SECTION = "关联"

/** 状态的中文名（写回时排优先级用） */
const STATUS_PRIORITY = {
  verified: 0,
  draft: 1,
  rejected: 2,
  todo: 3,
}

/**
 * 今天的日期（YYYY-MM-DD）。reviewed_at 用它。
 * @param {Date} [date]
 * @returns {string}
 */
function todayStamp(date) {
  const d = date instanceof Date ? date : new Date()
  const y = d.getFullYear()
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")
  return y + "-" + m + "-" + day
}

/**
 * 挑出待抽检的单元：还没被人看过（reviewed 不为 true）的单元。
 * 排序：已验证优先，其次待校验，再次待补内容、已打回；同级按 id 升序。
 * @param {object[]} units
 * @param {{sample?: number, track?: string}} opts
 * @returns {object[]}
 */
function pickReviewTargets(units, opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const only = o.track ? String(o.track) : ""
  const list = (Array.isArray(units) ? units : [])
    .filter(function (unit) {
      if (only !== "" && String(unit.track || "") !== only) return false
      return unit.reviewed !== true
    })
    .slice()
    .sort(function (a, b) {
      const pa = STATUS_PRIORITY[String(a.status || "todo")] === undefined ? 9 : STATUS_PRIORITY[String(a.status || "todo")]
      const pb = STATUS_PRIORITY[String(b.status || "todo")] === undefined ? 9 : STATUS_PRIORITY[String(b.status || "todo")]
      if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb
      const ia = String(a.id || "")
      const ib = String(b.id || "")
      return ia < ib ? -1 : ia > ib ? 1 : 0
    })
  const sample = Number(o.sample) > 0 ? Number(o.sample) : list.length
  return list.slice(0, sample)
}

/**
 * 写回抽检结论。
 * @param {{unit: object, passed: boolean, note?: string,
 *          unitsDir: string, repoRoot: string, stateFile?: string|null, today?: string}} opts
 * @returns {{ok: boolean, code: string, changed: boolean, reason: string, diff: string,
 *            unit: {id: string, status: string, reviewed: boolean, reviewed_at: string}|null}}
 */
function applyReview(opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const unit = o.unit || {}
  const id = String(unit.id || "")
  const passed = o.passed === true
  const note = String(o.note || "").trim()
  const unitsDir = o.unitsDir || ""
  const repoRoot = o.repoRoot || ""
  const today = String(o.today || todayStamp())

  function fail(code, reason) {
    return { ok: false, code: code, changed: false, reason: reason, diff: "", unit: null }
  }

  if (unitsDir === "" || repoRoot === "") {
    return fail("invalid", "还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用")
  }
  const file = unitFilePath(unitsDir, unit)
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
    return fail("missing", "单元文件不存在：" + file)
  }

  const before = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")
  const patch = { reviewed: true, reviewed_at: today }
  if (!passed) patch.status = "rejected"

  let after = ""
  try {
    after = applyFrontmatterPatch(before, patch)
    if (note !== "") after = appendSectionNote(after, REVIEW_SECTION, "抽检意见：" + note)
  } catch (err) {
    return fail("invalid", String((err && err.message) || err))
  }

  const written = writeFileChecked(file, after, {
    repoRoot: repoRoot,
    stateFile: o.stateFile || null,
  })
  if (!written.ok) {
    return fail("blocked", written.reason)
  }

  const matched = /^status:[ \t]*(.+)$/m.exec(after)
  const status = matched
    ? String(matched[1]).trim()
    : passed
      ? String(unit.status || "verified")
      : "rejected"
  return {
    ok: true,
    code: "ok",
    changed: written.changed,
    reason: "",
    diff: written.diff,
    unit: { id: id, status: status, reviewed: true, reviewed_at: today },
  }
}

module.exports = {
  REVIEW_SECTION: REVIEW_SECTION,
  STATUS_PRIORITY: STATUS_PRIORITY,
  todayStamp: todayStamp,
  pickReviewTargets: pickReviewTargets,
  applyReview: applyReview,
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5：实现 `tools/supply.js`**

新建 `tools/supply.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply.js —— 供给流水线 CLI（契约 §10）
 *
 *   node tools/supply.js plan    [--track <t>] [--limit <n>]
 *   node tools/supply.js generate [--track <t>] [--limit <n>] [--session <batchId>]
 *   node tools/supply.js verify  [--all | --track <t> | --unit <id>]
 *   node tools/supply.js review  [--sample <n>] [--track <t>]
 *   node tools/supply.js report  [--track <t>]
 *
 * 三条纪律：
 *   1. **离线批处理**（G30）：不依赖 HTTP 服务。自己开 SQLite、自己读 learning 仓
 *      （路径一律经 core/paths.js 的 resolveLearningRoot 解析，绝不硬编码）。
 *   2. **不自动提交**（G13）：写回 learning 仓只打印 diff，绝不执行 git commit / git push。
 *   3. **密钥只从环境变量读**（G9）：本 CLI **不接受** --api-key 这类参数。
 *
 * LLM 关掉时（G10）：只有 generate 受影响（退出码 2 + 中文提示）；
 * plan / verify / review / report 都不调 LLM，照常可用。
 */

const fs = require("node:fs")
const path = require("node:path")
const readline = require("node:readline/promises")

const { resolveLearningRoot, localBankDir } = require("../core/paths.js")
const { TRACK_CODES, labelOf } = require("../core/track/tracks.js")
const { loadUnits } = require("../core/unit/index.js")
const { createLlmClient } = require("../core/llm/client.js")
const { createLlmService, readLlmSettings } = require("../core/llm/service.js")
const { FileProvider } = require("../core/bank/provider.js")
const { openDb, createStore } = require("../server/db.js")
const { scanGaps, writePlanFiles, missingOf } = require("../core/supply/plan.js")
const { verifyBatch } = require("../core/supply/verify.js")
const { stateFilePath } = require("../core/supply/unit-file.js")
const { pickReviewTargets, applyReview, todayStamp } = require("../core/supply/review.js")

/** app 根目录（projects/apps/study-hub） */
const APP_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..")

/** 五个子命令（契约 §10） */
const COMMANDS = ["plan", "generate", "verify", "review", "report"]

/** 退出码（契约 §10.1） */
const EXIT_OK = 0
const EXIT_USAGE = 1
const EXIT_LLM_OFF = 2

/** 每个子命令允许的选项 */
const ALLOWED_OPTIONS = {
  plan: ["track", "limit"],
  generate: ["track", "limit", "session"],
  verify: ["all", "track", "unit"],
  review: ["sample", "track"],
  report: ["track"],
}

/** 布尔开关（不带值） */
const BOOLEAN_OPTIONS = ["all"]

/** 需要取值的选项 */
const VALUE_OPTIONS = ["track", "limit", "session", "unit", "sample"]

/** 不许出现在命令行上的词（密钥纪律，G9） */
const SECRET_WORDS = ["key", "token", "secret", "password", "passwd"]

/** 缺口种类的中文名 */
const GAP_LABELS = {
  asserts: "缺断言脚本",
  statement: "缺讲解正文",
  cases: "缺测试用例",
  solution: "缺参考解法",
}

/** 单元状态的中文名 */
const STATUS_LABELS = {
  todo: "待补内容",
  draft: "待校验",
  verified: "已验证",
  rejected: "已打回",
  skipped: "已跳过",
}

const USAGE = [
  "供给流水线 CLI（契约 §10）",
  "",
  "用法：node tools/supply.js <子命令> [选项]",
  "",
  "  plan     [--track <轨道>] [--limit <n>]        扫描缺口清单，写 learning/units/<轨道>/plan.json",
  "  generate [--track <轨道>] [--limit <n>] [--session <批次号>]   调 LLM 补断言脚本与讲解正文",
  "  verify   [--all | --track <轨道> | --unit <单元id>]          跑执行器反向校验与变异测试",
  "  review   [--sample <n>] [--track <轨道>]      抽检并写回 frontmatter",
  "  report   [--track <轨道>]                    打印每轨计数与欠账",
  "",
  "退出码：0 正常；1 参数错或 learning 仓路径解析不出来；2 LLM 关掉",
].join("\n")

/**
 * 解析命令行参数。
 * @param {string[]} argv
 * @returns {{command: string, options: object, error: string}}
 */
function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = Array.isArray(argv) ? argv.slice() : []
  const options = {}
  let command = ""
  let error = ""
  let i = 0

  while (i < args.length) {
    const arg = String(args[i])
    if (arg === "--help" || arg === "-h") {
      return { command: "help", options: options, error: "" }
    }
    if (arg.slice(0, 2) === "--") {
      const eq = arg.indexOf("=")
      const name = eq === -1 ? arg.slice(2) : arg.slice(2, eq)
      if (name === "") {
        error = "选项名是空的"
        break
      }
      const lower = name.toLowerCase()
      const isSecret = SECRET_WORDS.some(function (word) {
        return lower.indexOf(word) !== -1
      })
      if (isSecret) {
        error = "不接受把密钥写在命令行上：" + arg + "（密钥只从环境变量或 .env 里读）"
        break
      }
      if (BOOLEAN_OPTIONS.indexOf(name) !== -1) {
        options[name] = true
        i += 1
        continue
      }
      if (VALUE_OPTIONS.indexOf(name) === -1) {
        error = "不认识的选项：" + arg
        break
      }
      let value = eq === -1 ? "" : arg.slice(eq + 1)
      if (eq === -1) {
        i += 1
        if (i >= args.length) {
          error = "选项 " + arg + " 后面缺值"
          break
        }
        value = String(args[i])
      }
      options[name] = value
      i += 1
      continue
    }
    if (command === "") {
      command = arg
      i += 1
      continue
    }
    error = "多了一个位置参数：" + arg
    break
  }

  if (error !== "") return { command: "", options: options, error: error }
  if (command === "") {
    return { command: "", options: options, error: "少了一个子命令（可用：" + COMMANDS.join(" / ") + "）" }
  }
  if (COMMANDS.indexOf(command) === -1) {
    return {
      command: "",
      options: options,
      error: "不认识的子命令：" + command + "（可用：" + COMMANDS.join(" / ") + "）",
    }
  }
  const allowed = ALLOWED_OPTIONS[command] || []
  Object.keys(options).forEach(function (name) {
    if (error !== "") return
    if (allowed.indexOf(name) === -1) {
      error =
        "「" + command + "」不接受选项 --" + name +
        "（可用：" + allowed.map(function (n) { return "--" + n }).join(" / ") + "）"
    }
  })
  if (error !== "") return { command: "", options: options, error: error }
  return { command: command, options: options, error: "" }
}

/**
 * 解析正整数选项。
 * @param {string|undefined} raw
 * @param {number} fallback 缺省值
 * @returns {number|null} null = 不合法
 */
function parseCount(raw, fallback) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw) === "") return fallback
  const text = String(raw).trim()
  if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(text)) return null
  const n = Number(text)
  if (n <= 0) return null
  return n
}

/**
 * 截一段文字出来给人看。
 * @param {string} text
 * @param {number} limit
 * @returns {string}
 */
function preview(text, limit) {
  const s = String(text || "").trim()
  const n = Number(limit) > 0 ? Number(limit) : 400
  if (s.length <= n) return s
  return s.slice(0, n) + "……（后面还有 " + (s.length - n) + " 字）"
}

/**
 * 统计每种缺口各有几个。
 * @param {object[]} gaps
 * @returns {object}
 */
function countKinds(gaps) {
  const counts = {}
  ;(Array.isArray(gaps) ? gaps : []).forEach(function (gap) {
    ;(gap.missing || []).forEach(function (kind) {
      counts[kind] = (counts[kind] || 0) + 1
    })
  })
  return counts
}

/**
 * 默认的人工抽检问询：终端里问人。
 * @param {object} unit
 * @returns {Promise<{passed: boolean, note: string}|null>} null = 人要退出
 */
async function defaultAsk(unit) {
  const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: process.stdin, output: process.stderr })
  try {
    const answer = await rl.question(
      "单元 " + String(unit.id || "") + "（" + String(unit.title || "") + "）通过吗？输入 y 通过，n 打回，q 退出："
    )
    const a = String(answer || "").trim().toLowerCase()
    if (a === "q" || a === "quit") return null
    const passed = a === "" || a === "y" || a === "yes"
    const note = await rl.question("抽检意见（可留空）：")
    return { passed: passed, note: String(note || "").trim() }
  } finally {
    rl.close()
  }
}

/**
 * 读单元的断言脚本。
 * @param {string} learningRoot
 * @param {object} unit
 * @returns {{file: string, text: string}|null}
 */
function readScriptText(learningRoot, unit) {
  const rel = String((unit.entry && unit.entry.script) || "")
  if (rel === "") return null
  const file = path.join(learningRoot, rel.split("/").join(path.sep))
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return null
  return { file: file, text: fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8") }
}

/**
 * 挑要补断言脚本的单元：卡类、还缺断言脚本的。
 * @param {object[]} units
 * @param {{track?: string, limit?: number, learningRoot: string|null, contentRoots: string[]}} opts
 * @returns {object[]}
 */
function pickGenerateTargets(units, opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const only = o.track ? String(o.track) : ""
  const list = (Array.isArray(units) ? units : []).filter(function (unit) {
    if (only !== "" && String(unit.track || "") !== only) return false
    if (String(unit.kind || "") !== "card") return false
    const missing = missingOf(unit, {
      learningRoot: o.learningRoot || null,
      contentRoots: o.contentRoots || [],
      warnings: [],
    })
    return missing.indexOf("asserts") !== -1
  })
  const n = Number(o.limit) > 0 ? Number(o.limit) : list.length
  return list.slice(0, n)
}

/**
 * 跑一次 CLI。
 * @param {string[]} argv
 * @param {{env?: object, client?: object|null, learningRoot?: string|null, localBank?: string|null,
 *          dbPath?: string, stateFile?: string|null, ask?: Function, today?: Function}} ctx
 * @returns {Promise<{code: number, stdout: string, stderr: string}>}
 */
async function runCli(argv, ctx) {
  const c = ctx || {}
  const env = c.env || process.env
  const out = []
  const err = []

  function say(text) {
    out.push(String(text))
  }
  function warn(text) {
    err.push(String(text))
  }
  function done(code) {
    return {
      code: code,
      stdout: out.length ? out.join("\n") + "\n" : "",
      stderr: err.length ? err.join("\n") + "\n" : "",
    }
  }

  const parsed = parseArgs(argv)
  if (parsed.error !== "") {
    warn(parsed.error)
    warn(USAGE)
    return done(EXIT_USAGE)
  }
  if (parsed.command === "help") {
    say(USAGE)
    return done(EXIT_OK)
  }

  const command = parsed.command
  const options = parsed.options

  // ---- 轨道与选项校验 ----
  const track = options.track ? String(options.track) : ""
  if (track !== "" && TRACK_CODES.indexOf(track) === -1) {
    warn("不认识的轨道：" + track + "（可用：" + TRACK_CODES.join(" / ") + "）")
    return done(EXIT_USAGE)
  }
  const limit = parseCount(options.limit, 0)
  if (limit === null) {
    warn("--limit 必须是正整数")
    return done(EXIT_USAGE)
  }
  const sample = parseCount(options.sample, 5)
  if (sample === null) {
    warn("--sample 必须是正整数")
    return done(EXIT_USAGE)
  }
  // ---- 解析 learning 仓（G16：解析不到就降级，不抛错） ----
  const learning = resolveLearningRoot(env, c.learningRoot === undefined ? null : c.learningRoot)
  const localBank = c.localBank === null ? null : localBankDir(env, c.localBank || null)
  const unitsDir = learning.root ? path.join(learning.root, "units") : null
  const grokkingDir = learning.root ? path.join(learning.root, "scaffold", "grokking") : null
  const contentRoots = []
  if (learning.root) contentRoots.push(learning.root)
  if (localBank) contentRoots.push(localBank)
  const stateFile = c.stateFile || stateFilePath(APP_ROOT)

  let units = []
  let loadWarnings = []
  try {
    const loaded = loadUnits({
      learningRoot: learning.root,
      localBank: localBank,
      unitsDir: unitsDir,
      grokkingDir: grokkingDir,
    })
    units = loaded.units || []
    loadWarnings = loaded.warnings || []
  } catch (e) {
    warn("读单元列表失败：" + String((e && e.message) || e))
  }
  if (!learning.ok) {
    warn(learning.reason || "还没连上 learning 仓，进入纯本地模式")
  }

  const scan = scanGaps({
    units: units,
    learningRoot: learning.root,
    contentRoots: contentRoots,
    track: track,
  })

  // ---- plan ----
  if (command === "plan") {
    say("供给缺口清单（" + todayStamp() + "）")
    scan.tracks.forEach(function (item) {
      const code = String(item.track || "")
      say("")
      say(labelOf(code) + "（" + code + "）：共 " + item.total + " 个单元 —— 已验证 " + item.verified
        + "、待补 " + (item.todo + item.draft) + "、已打回 " + item.rejected + "、已抽检 " + item.reviewed)
      const gaps = scan.gaps.filter(function (gap) {
        return String(gap.track) === code
      })
      if (gaps.length === 0) {
        say("  没有缺口")
        return
      }
      const shown = limit > 0 ? gaps.slice(0, limit) : gaps
      say("  缺口 " + gaps.length + " 个：")
      shown.forEach(function (gap) {
        const kinds = (gap.missing || []).map(function (kind) {
          return GAP_LABELS[kind] || kind
        })
        say("    " + gap.id + "  " + gap.title + "  缺：" + kinds.join("、"))
      })
      if (shown.length < gaps.length) {
        say("    ……还有 " + (gaps.length - shown.length) + " 个没列出来")
      }
    })
    if (scan.warnings.length > 0) {
      say("")
      say("提醒：")
      scan.warnings.forEach(function (text) {
        say("  " + text)
      })
    }
    loadWarnings.forEach(function (text) {
      warn("加载单元时的提醒：" + text)
    })
    if (!unitsDir) {
      warn("还没连上 learning 仓，plan.json 写不了（缺口清单只打在屏幕上）")
    } else {
      const written = writePlanFiles({
        result: scan,
        unitsDir: unitsDir,
        repoRoot: learning.root,
        stateFile: stateFile,
      })
      written.written.forEach(function (code) {
        say("已写入：" + path.join(unitsDir, code, "plan.json"))
      })
      written.blocked.forEach(function (code) {
        warn("缺口清单没写进 " + path.join(unitsDir, code, "plan.json") + "：" + written.reason)
      })
    }
    return done(EXIT_OK)
  }

  // ---- report ----
  if (command === "report") {
    say("供给报告（" + todayStamp() + "）")
    say("learning 仓：" + (learning.root ? learning.root : "还没连上 learning 仓（纯本地模式）"))
    scan.tracks.forEach(function (item) {
      say(labelOf(String(item.track || "")) + "：共 " + item.total + " 个单元 —— todo " + item.todo
        + " / draft " + item.draft + " / verified " + item.verified
        + " / rejected " + item.rejected + " / 已抽检 " + item.reviewed)
    })
    const kinds = countKinds(scan.gaps)
    const names = Object.keys(kinds)
    say("欠账：" + (names.length === 0
      ? "没有"
      : names.map(function (kind) {
          return (GAP_LABELS[kind] || kind) + " " + kinds[kind] + " 个"
        }).join("、")))
    loadWarnings.forEach(function (text) {
      warn("加载单元时的提醒：" + text)
    })
    return done(EXIT_OK)
  }

  // ---- generate ----
  if (command === "generate") {
    if (!learning.root || !unitsDir) {
      warn("还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用：" + (learning.reason || "解析不出 learning 仓路径"))
      return done(EXIT_USAGE)
    }
    const db = openDb(c.dbPath || path.join(APP_ROOT, "data", "app.db"))
    const store = createStore(db)
    try {
      const fixedClient = c.client || null
      const service = createLlmService({
        client: fixedClient,
        getClient: fixedClient
          ? undefined
          : function () {
              const s = readLlmSettings(store)
              return createLlmClient({
                provider: s.provider || undefined,
                model: s.model || undefined,
                baseUrl: s.baseUrl || undefined,
                enabled: s.enabled,
                env: env,
              })
            },
        store: store,
        provider: new FileProvider(contentRoots),
        env: env,
        learningRoot: learning.root,
        unitsDir: unitsDir,
        stateFile: stateFile,
      })
      const client = service.currentClient()
      if (client.name === "null") {
        warn("LLM 没开（" + String(client.reason || "未启用") + "），generate 用不了。")
        warn("plan / verify / review / report 都不调 LLM，照常可用。")
        return done(EXIT_LLM_OFF)
      }
      if (track === "algorithm") {
        // P4 的边界只有 DB/AI 两轨（边界口径第 1 条），算法轨的批量生成是 P5 的活。
        // 这里**不报「参数错」**——这一期只是没有活干，正常退出即可。
        // verify 不拦：算法单元由 core/supply/verify.js 逐个跳过（理由「算法题由 P5 校验」）。
        say("算法轨的批量生成是 P5 的活（--track algorithm 不在本期范围），没有要补的单元")
        return done(EXIT_OK)
      }
      const batchId = String(options.session || "supply")
      const targets = pickGenerateTargets(units, {
        track: track,
        limit: limit > 0 ? limit : 10,
        learningRoot: learning.root,
        contentRoots: contentRoots,
      })
      if (targets.length === 0) {
        say("没有要补的单元（卡类的断言脚本都齐了）")
        return done(EXIT_OK)
      }
      say("本次要补 " + targets.length + " 个单元（批次号：" + batchId + "）")
      let ok = 0
      let failed = 0
      for (const unit of targets) {
        say("—— " + unit.id + " " + unit.title + " ——")
        try {
          const asserted = await service.generateAssert({ unit: unit, batchId: batchId })
          say("  断言脚本已生成：" + path.relative(learning.root, asserted.file))
          const explained = await service.writeExplain({ unit: unit, batchId: batchId })
          say("  讲解正文已写入：" + path.relative(learning.root, explained.file))
          ok += 1
        } catch (e) {
          const status = e && e.status
          if (status === 503) {
            warn("LLM 中途断了（" + String((e && e.message) || e) + "），剩下的单元不再处理")
            break
          }
          if (status === 429) {
            warn("配额用完了（" + String((e && e.message) || e) + "），剩下的单元不再处理")
            break
          }
          warn("  单元 " + unit.id + " 生成失败：" + String((e && e.message) || e))
          failed += 1
        }
      }
      say("")
      say("本次生成：" + ok + " 个成功，" + failed + " 个失败")
      return done(EXIT_OK)
    } finally {
      db.close()
    }
  }

  // ---- verify ----
  if (command === "verify") {
    const hasAll = options.all === true
    const unitId = options.unit ? String(options.unit) : ""
    if (!hasAll && unitId === "" && track === "") {
      warn("verify 至少要一个范围：--all / --track <轨道> / --unit <单元id>")
      return done(EXIT_USAGE)
    }
    let targets = units
    if (unitId !== "") {
      targets = targets.filter(function (unit) {
        return String(unit.id) === unitId
      })
      if (targets.length === 0) {
        warn("找不到单元：" + unitId)
        return done(EXIT_USAGE)
      }
    }
    if (track !== "") {
      targets = targets.filter(function (unit) {
        return String(unit.track) === track
      })
    }
    if (!learning.root) {
      warn("还没连上 learning 仓，没有单元可校验")
      return done(EXIT_OK)
    }
    const batch = await verifyBatch({
      units: targets,
      learningRoot: learning.root,
      unitsDir: unitsDir,
      repoRoot: learning.root,
      stateFile: stateFile,
    })
    say("校验了 " + batch.counts.total + " 个单元：通过 " + batch.counts.verified
      + "，打回 " + batch.counts.rejected + "，跳过 " + batch.counts.skipped)
    batch.results.forEach(function (item) {
      say("  " + item.id + "  " + (STATUS_LABELS[item.status] || item.status) + "  " + item.reason)
    })
    if (batch.diffs.length > 0) {
      say("")
      say("下面是改动（自己看着提交，CLI 绝不替你提交）：")
      batch.diffs.forEach(function (diff) {
        say(diff)
      })
    }
    return done(EXIT_OK)
  }

  // ---- review ----
  if (command === "review") {
    if (!learning.root || !unitsDir) {
      warn("还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用：" + (learning.reason || "解析不出 learning 仓路径"))
      return done(EXIT_USAGE)
    }
    const ask = typeof c.ask === "function" ? c.ask : defaultAsk
    const targets = pickReviewTargets(units, { sample: sample, track: track })
    if (targets.length === 0) {
      say("没有待抽检的单元（都被人看过了）")
      return done(EXIT_OK)
    }
    say("待抽检单元 " + targets.length + " 个（输入 y 通过，n 打回，q 提前退出）：")
    let written = 0
    let blocked = 0
    for (const unit of targets) {
      say("")
      say("—— " + unit.id + " " + unit.title + " ——")
      say("题面：" + preview(unit.statement, 400))
      const script = readScriptText(learning.root, unit)
      if (script) {
        say("断言脚本：" + path.relative(learning.root, script.file))
        say(preview(script.text, 400))
      } else {
        say("断言脚本：还没有")
      }
      const answer = await ask(unit)
      if (!answer) {
        say("提前退出，已经抽检过的单元保留")
        break
      }
      const applied = applyReview({
        unit: unit,
        passed: answer.passed === true,
        note: String(answer.note || ""),
        unitsDir: unitsDir,
        repoRoot: learning.root,
        stateFile: stateFile,
        today: todayStamp(),
      })
      if (!applied.ok) {
        warn("写回被阻塞：" + applied.reason)
        blocked += 1
        continue
      }
      say("已写回 " + applied.unit.id + "：reviewed: true，reviewed_at: " + applied.unit.reviewed_at
        + "，status: " + applied.unit.status)
      if (applied.diff) {
        say("（下面是 diff，自己看着提交）")
        say(applied.diff)
      }
      written += 1
    }
    say("")
    say("本次抽检：写回 " + written + " 个，没写成 " + blocked + " 个")
    return done(EXIT_OK)
  }

  warn("没实现的子命令：" + command)
  return done(EXIT_USAGE)
}

/**
 * 命令行入口。
 * @param {string[]} argv
 * @returns {Promise<number>}
 */
async function main(argv) {
  const res = await runCli(argv, {})
  if (res.stdout) process.stdout.write(res.stdout)
  if (res.stderr) process.stderr.write(res.stderr)
  return res.code
}

module.exports = {
  COMMANDS: COMMANDS,
  EXIT_OK: EXIT_OK,
  EXIT_USAGE: EXIT_USAGE,
  EXIT_LLM_OFF: EXIT_LLM_OFF,
  USAGE: USAGE,
  parseArgs: parseArgs,
  pickGenerateTargets: pickGenerateTargets,
  runCli: runCli,
  main: main,
}

if (require.main === module) {
  main(process.argv.slice(2)).then(function (code) {
    process.exitCode = code
  })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6：跑测试，确认通过**

```bash
cd projects/apps/study-hub && node tests/supply-cli.test.js
```

**Expected（通过）**（下面是**实跑**出来的数字，机器不同毫秒数会有出入，条目与条数必须一致）：

```
✔ CLI 的子命令只有五个 (0.7803ms)
✔ parseArgs：认得五个子命令 (0.2106ms)
✔ parseArgs：不接受把密钥写在命令行上（G9） (0.0993ms)
✔ parseArgs：不认识的子命令 / 不该给这个子命令的选项都要报错 (0.0759ms)
✔ plan：打出缺口清单，并写 learning/units/<track>/plan.json (197.0655ms)
✔ plan：纯本地模式打印中文提示并正常退出（G17） (0.1818ms)
✔ generate：LLM 关掉时退出码 2 + 中文提示（G10） (170.3950ms)
✔ generate：LLM 开着时生成断言脚本 + 讲解正文，状态是 draft (230.1452ms)
✔ generate：没连上 learning 仓时退出码 1 (0.1798ms)
✔ verify：draft → verified，只打印 diff，不提交（G13） (762.2283ms)
✔ verify：变异抓不住 → 打回 rejected (590.7022ms)
✔ review：写回 reviewed: true 与 reviewed_at (205.7747ms)
✔ review：别人动过的文件一律阻塞写回（G14） (214.1832ms)
✔ report：打印每轨计数与欠账 (171.1753ms)
ℹ tests 14
ℹ suites 0
ℹ pass 14
ℹ fail 0
```

> **`verify` 那两条为什么慢**：每条都要真起一个 Python 进程跑一遍断言脚本、再起一个跑一遍变异体——几百毫秒是正常的。
>
> **本机没有 Python 时**：`verify` 那两条会变成「跳过」（结论是 `skipped`，状态不变），其余照常通过。

### Task 9: 供给接口 + 供给页（`server/api-supply.js`、`web/pages/supply.js`）

**Files:**
- 新建 `server/api-supply.js`
- 新建 `web/pages/supply.js`
- 改 `server/app.js`（装配供给路由 + 加载单元列表 + 多一个 `stateFile` 选项）
- 改 `web/app.js`（`TABS` 核对 + 把 `TABS` 导出去给测试用）
- 改 `web/index.html`（加 `<script src="/pages/supply.js">`）
- 新建 `tests/supply-api.test.js`
- 新建 `tests/supply-web.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `core/supply/plan.js`（**Task 6 产出**）：`scanGaps(opts) : {tracks, gaps, warnings}`
  - `core/supply/review.js`（**Task 8 产出**）：`applyReview(opts) : {ok, code, changed, reason, diff, unit}`
  - `core/supply/unit-file.js`（**Task 4 产出**）：`unitFilePath(unitsDir, unit)`
  - `core/unit/index.js`（**P0 交付**）：`loadUnits(opts) : {units, warnings, byId}`
  - `server/router.js`（**P0 交付**）：`readJsonBody(req) : Promise<object>`（非法 JSON 抛 `HttpError(400)`，超过 1MB 抛 `HttpError(413)`）
  - `web/ui.js`（**P0 交付**，**不改**）：`pageHead` / `hero` / `panel` / `statGrid` / `pill` / `empty` —— className 分别是 `"page-head"` / `"panel hero"` / `"panel"` / `"panel quiet"` / `"stat-grid"` / `"pill"` / `"empty"`
- Produces:
  - `server/api-supply.js`：`buildSupplyRoutes(ctx) : Array<{method, pattern, handler}>`
    - `GET /api/supply/status` → **200** `{tracks, gaps, warnings}`
    - `POST /api/supply/review/:id` → **200** `{ok: true, unit: {id, status, reviewed, reviewed_at}}`；**404** `{"error":"单元不存在","id":"<id>"}`；**400** `{"error":"passed 必须是布尔值"}`；**409**（别人动过文件，写回被阻塞）`{"error":"<中文理由>"}`；**503**（纯本地模式）`{"error":"还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用"}`
  - `web/pages/supply.js`：`window.StudyHub.pages.supply = {title: "供给", render: async function (view) {}}`；Node 下另导出纯函数 `buildSupplyView(status) : HTMLElement[]`

**四层信息层次（G7）**——供给页必须长这样（className **精确匹配**，这是 `tests/ui-hierarchy.test.js` 的判定方式）：

| 层 | 构件 | className |
|---|---|---|
| 页头（**有且只有一个**） | `UI.pageHead({eyebrow, title, sub})` | `"page-head"`（内含 `"eyebrow"` / `"page-title"` / `"page-sub"`） |
| 主行动 | `UI.hero({title, sub, children})` | `"panel hero"` |
| 数字 | `UI.statGrid(items)` | `"stat-grid"` |
| 普通卡 | `UI.panel({title, children})` | `"panel"` |
| 背景信息 | `UI.panel({title, children, className: "quiet"})` | `"panel quiet"` |
| 状态 | `UI.pill(text)` | `"pill"`（**不带色调后缀**，className 才精确等于 `"pill"`；文字一律中文） |

> **为什么药丸不传色调**：`UI.pill(text, tone)` 的 className 是 `"pill" + (tone ? " " + tone : "")`，带色调就变成 `"pill ok"`，`tests/ui-hierarchy.test.js` 那种「className 精确相等」的查找就找不到它了。所以供给页的状态药丸一律 `UI.pill(text)`——颜色由 CSS 的默认样式承载，语义由中文文字承载（G7：「状态用 `.pill` / `.badge` 且必须带中文文字」）。

**纯本地模式（G17）**：`GET /api/supply/status` 返回 **200** + `{tracks: [], gaps: [], warnings: ["还没连上 learning 仓，供给状态只能看本地数据，写回不可用"]}`——**不许白屏**；`POST /api/supply/review/:id` 返回 **503** `{"error":"还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用"}`。

**抽检入口的口径**：契约 §8.4 只给了一个读接口（`status`），它的 `gaps` 里带着 `id` 与 `title`，正好够人点「通过 / 打回」。所以**供给页的抽检入口 = 对缺口清单里的单元当场给结论**（通过 → `reviewed: true`；打回 → `status: rejected`）；**不新增读接口，也不改 §8.4 的响应形状**（保持 `{tracks, gaps, warnings}` 三个字段，与 G17 的纯本地响应完全一致）。命令行那边的 `review --sample <n>` 抽的是「还没被人看过」的单元（见 Task 8），两者写回走的是**同一份** `core/supply/review.js`。

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply-api.test.js —— 供给接口（契约 §8.4）
 *
 * 真起一个 HTTP 服务打过去；learning 仓是临时目录里的真 git 仓。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")
const { execFileSync } = require("node:child_process")

const { createApp } = require("../server/app.js")

/** 契约 §3.3 的 loader C 单元文件 */
const UNIT_FILE = [
  "---",
  "id: db:demo",
  "track: database",
  "kind: card",
  "title: 演示卡",
  "difficulty: medium",
  "topics: []",
  "source: generated",
  "language: python",
  "demo: scaffold/grokking/demo/main.py",
  'reference: ""',
  "graded: script",
  "script: units/database/demo.assert.py",
  "status: draft",
  "reviewed: false",
  'reviewed_at: ""',
  "subtracks: []",
  "---",
  "",
  "## 讲解",
  "",
  "演示卡的讲解正文。",
  "",
  "## 关联",
  "",
  "- 轮次：R37 Grokking 数据库",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 造一个假的 learning 仓 */
function makeRepo() {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-api-repo-"))
  execFileSync("git", ["init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.email", "t@t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.name", "t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const cardDir = path.join(root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo")
  fs.mkdirSync(cardDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "main.py"), "print('hi')\n", "utf8")
  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units")
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database"), { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), UNIT_FILE, "utf8")
  execFileSync("git", ["add", "-A"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["commit", "-m", "init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const stateDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-api-state-"))
  return { root: root, unitsDir: unitsDir, stateFile: path.join(stateDir, "self-written.json") }
}

/** 起一个服务。repo 为 null = 纯本地模式（没连上 learning 仓） */
async function withApp(repo, fn) {
  const app = createApp({
    dbPath: ":memory:",
    learningRoot: repo ? repo.root : null,
    localBank: null,
    env: {},
    // 「自己写过」清单指到临时目录——测试不许往仓库里写东西
    stateFile: repo ? repo.stateFile : null,
  })
  const port = await app.listen(0)
  try {
    await fn("http://127.0.0.1:" + port, app)
  } finally {
    await app.close()
  }
}

test("GET /api/supply/status：返回每轨计数、缺口与提醒", async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo()
  await withApp(repo, async function (base) {
    const res = await fetch(base + "/api/supply/status")
    assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200)
    const body = await res.json()
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(body.tracks), JSON.stringify(body))
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(body.gaps), JSON.stringify(body))
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(body.warnings), JSON.stringify(body))
    const db = body.tracks.find(function (t) { return t.track === "database" })
    assert.ok(db, JSON.stringify(body.tracks))
    assert.strictEqual(db.total, 1)
  })
})

  const repo = makeRepo()
  await withApp(repo, async function (base) {
    const res = await fetch(base + "/api/supply/status?track=database")
    assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200)
    const body = await res.json()
    assert.strictEqual(body.tracks.length, 1)
    assert.strictEqual(body.tracks[0].track, "database")
  })
})

test("纯本地模式：status 仍返回 200 + 空数据，不白屏（G17）", async function () {
  await withApp(null, async function (base) {
    const res = await fetch(base + "/api/supply/status")
    assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200)
    const body = await res.json()
    assert.deepStrictEqual(body.tracks, [])
    assert.deepStrictEqual(body.gaps, [])
    assert.ok(body.warnings.length > 0, JSON.stringify(body))
    assert.ok(body.warnings[0].indexOf("learning") !== -1, JSON.stringify(body.warnings))
  })
})

test("POST /api/supply/review/:id：写回 reviewed 与 reviewed_at", async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo()
  await withApp(repo, async function (base) {
    const res = await fetch(base + "/api/supply/review/db:demo", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ passed: true, note: "讲得清楚" }),
    })
    assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200)
    const body = await res.json()
    assert.strictEqual(body.ok, true)
    assert.strictEqual(body.unit.id, "db:demo")
    assert.strictEqual(body.unit.reviewed, true)
    assert.ok(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(body.unit.reviewed_at), JSON.stringify(body))
    const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo.unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), "utf8")
    assert.ok(text.indexOf("reviewed: true") !== -1, text)
    assert.ok(text.indexOf("讲得清楚") !== -1, text)
  })
})

  await withApp(repo, async function (base) {

test("别人动过的文件 → 409，写回被阻塞（G14）", async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo()
  const file = path.join(repo.unitsDir, "database", "demo.md")
  fs.writeFileSync(file, fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8") + "\n别人加的一行\n", "utf8")
  await withApp(repo, async function (base) {
    const res = await fetch(base + "/api/supply/review/db:demo", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ passed: true }),
    })
    assert.strictEqual(res.status, 409)
    const body = await res.json()
    assert.ok(String(body.error).indexOf("写回已阻塞") !== -1, JSON.stringify(body))
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").indexOf("reviewed: true") === -1)
  })
})
```

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply-web.test.js —— 供给页的纯函数与四层信息层次
 *
 * 用 tests/helpers/dom-stub.js 驱动真实的页面渲染代码，
 * 断言的结构与 tests/ui-hierarchy.test.js 一致（那个文件是回归闸门，不许改）。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")

const stub = require("./helpers/dom-stub.js")

// 必须在 require web/app.js 之前把 document 铺好
const dom = stub.createDocument()
global.document = dom.document
global.window = global

require("../web/app.js")
const supplyPage = require("../web/pages/supply.js")
const PAGES = global.window.StudyHub.pages

/** 在树里找出所有 class 精确匹配的节点 */
function findAllByClass(node, className) {
  const out = []
  function walk(n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.className === className) out.push(n)
    ;(n.children || []).forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(node)
  return out
}

/** 在树里找出第一个 class 精确匹配的节点 */
function findByClass(node, className) {
  return findAllByClass(node, className)[0] || null
}

/** 在树里找出所有指定标签的节点（按钮按 className 找不到——按钮的 class 是 "primary" 之类） */
function findAllByTag(node, tag) {
  const out = []
  function walk(n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.tagName === tag) out.push(n)
    ;(n.children || []).forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(node)
  return out
}

/** 把树里所有文本拼起来 */
function collectText(node) {
  let out = ""
  function walk(n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.textContent) out += " " + n.textContent
    ;(n.children || []).forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(node)
  return out
}

/** 铺一个假的 fetch */
function stubFetch(routes, posted) {
  global.fetch = async function (url, options) {
    const method = options && options.method ? options.method : "GET"
    if (method === "POST") {
      if (posted) posted.push({ url: url, body: options.body })
      return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async function () { return { ok: true, unit: { id: "db:demo", status: "verified", reviewed: true, reviewed_at: "2026-11-13" } } } }
    }
    const key = Object.keys(routes).find(function (prefix) {
      return String(url).indexOf(prefix) === 0
    })
    if (!key) {
      return { ok: false, status: 404, json: async function () { return { error: "没有这条路由" } } }
    }
    const body = routes[key]
    return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async function () { return body } }
  }
}

/** 造一个空容器 */
function mountView() {
  const node = dom.document.createElement("div")
  node.setAttribute("id", "view")
  return node
}

/** 一份够喂饱供给页的响应 */
const STATUS = {
  tracks: [
    { track: "algorithm", total: 0, todo: 0, draft: 0, verified: 0, rejected: 0, reviewed: 0 },
    { track: "database", total: 1, todo: 0, draft: 1, verified: 0, rejected: 0, reviewed: 0 },
    { track: "ai", total: 0, todo: 0, draft: 0, verified: 0, rejected: 0, reviewed: 0 },
  ],
  gaps: [
    { id: "db:demo", track: "database", kind: "card", title: "演示卡", missing: ["asserts", "statement"] },
  ],
  warnings: [],
}

})

  const hit = TABS.find(function (tab) { return tab.key === "supply" })
```

- [ ] **Step 4：实现 `server/api-supply.js`**

新建 `server/api-supply.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * api-supply.js —— P4 的供给接口（契约 §8.4）
 *
 *   GET  /api/supply/status      → {tracks, gaps, warnings}
 *   POST /api/supply/review/:id  → {ok: true, unit: {id, status, reviewed, reviewed_at}}
 *
 * 纯本地模式（没连上 learning 仓）：
 *   - status → 200 + {tracks: [], gaps: [], warnings: ["还没连上 learning 仓…"]}（G17，不许白屏）
 *   - review → 503 {"error":"还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用"}
 *
 * 路由在独立文件里，与 server/api.js 零冲突；由 server/app.js 合并进同一张路由表。
 */

const { readJsonBody } = require("./router.js")
const { scanGaps } = require("../core/supply/plan.js")
const { applyReview } = require("../core/supply/review.js")

/** 纯本地模式时的提醒 */
const LOCAL_WARNING = "还没连上 learning 仓，供给状态只能看本地数据，写回不可用"

/**
 * 组装供给路由。
 * @param {{store?: object, provider?: object, learning?: object, units?: object[],
 *          unitsById?: object, unitsDir?: string|null, stateFile?: string|null,
 *          contentRoots?: string[], env?: object, sendJson: Function}} ctx
 * @returns {Array<{method: string, pattern: string, handler: Function}>}
 */
function buildSupplyRoutes(ctx) {
  const c = ctx || {}
  return [
    {
      method: "GET",
      pattern: "/api/supply/status",
      handler: async function (req, res, params, query) {
        const learning = c.learning || { root: null, ok: false, reason: "" }
        if (!learning.ok || !learning.root) {
          c.sendJson(res, 200, { tracks: [], gaps: [], warnings: [LOCAL_WARNING] })
          return
        }
        const track = query.get("track") || ""
        const result = scanGaps({
          units: Array.isArray(c.units) ? c.units : [],
          learningRoot: learning.root,
          contentRoots: Array.isArray(c.contentRoots) ? c.contentRoots : [],
          track: track,
        })
        c.sendJson(res, 200, {
          tracks: result.tracks,
          gaps: result.gaps,
          warnings: result.warnings,
        })
      },
    },
    {
      method: "POST",
      pattern: "/api/supply/review/:id",
      handler: async function (req, res, params, query) {
        const learning = c.learning || { root: null, ok: false, reason: "" }
        if (!learning.ok || !learning.root) {
          c.sendJson(res, 503, { error: "还没连上 learning 仓，写回不可用" })
          return
        }
        const body = await readJsonBody(req)
        if (!body || typeof body.passed !== "boolean") {
          c.sendJson(res, 400, { error: "passed 必须是布尔值" })
          return
        }
        const id = String(params.id || "")
        const byId = c.unitsById || {}
        const unit = byId[id]
        if (!unit) {
          c.sendJson(res, 404, { error: "单元不存在", id: id })
          return
        }
        const applied = applyReview({
          unit: unit,
          passed: body.passed,
          note: String(body.note || ""),
          unitsDir: c.unitsDir || "",
          repoRoot: learning.root,
          stateFile: c.stateFile || null,
          today: typeof c.today === "function" ? c.today() : undefined,
        })
        if (!applied.ok) {
          const status = applied.code === "blocked" ? 409 : applied.code === "missing" ? 404 : 500
          c.sendJson(res, status, { error: applied.reason })
          return
        }
        c.sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, unit: applied.unit })
      },
    },
  ]
}

module.exports = { buildSupplyRoutes: buildSupplyRoutes, LOCAL_WARNING: LOCAL_WARNING }
```

新建 `web/pages/supply.js`：

```js
"use strict";
/**
 * supply.js —— 供给页（P4）
 *
 * 薄页，只摆三件事：
 *   1. 现在供给是什么状态（每轨的 todo / draft / verified / rejected）
 *   2. 还欠什么（缺口清单）
 *   3. 抽检入口：对缺口清单里的单元当场点「通过 / 打回」，写回 frontmatter
 *
 * 层次（G7）：一个页头 + 一张主卡（.panel hero）+ 数字块（.stat-grid）
 * + 普通卡（.panel）+ 背景卡（.panel quiet）。状态一律用带中文文字的药丸承载。
 * 不引路由框架（G29）：沿用「整页重绘 + renderSeq 竞态守卫」的机制。
 */

(function () {
  const A = window.StudyHub
  const el = A.el

  /**
   * 公共界面构件。浏览器里 ui.js 先加载挂 window.StudyHubUI；
   * Node 下没有全局挂载，就 require 同一份文件——实现只有一份。
   */
  const UI =
    (typeof window !== "undefined" && (window.StudyHubUI || (window.StudyHub && window.StudyHub.ui))) ||
    (typeof require === "function" ? require("../ui.js") : null)

  /** 缺口种类的中文名 */
  const GAP_LABELS = {
    asserts: "缺断言脚本",
    statement: "缺讲解正文",
    cases: "缺测试用例",
    solution: "缺参考解法",
  }

  /** 轨道的中文名（前端不引后端模块，这里写死一份） */
  const TRACK_LABELS = {
    algorithm: "算法",
    database: "数据库",
    ai: "AI",
  }

  /** 单元状态的中文名 */
  const STATUS_LABELS = {
    todo: "待补内容",
    draft: "待校验",
    verified: "已验证",
    rejected: "已打回",
  }

  /** 药丸：不传色调，className 才精确等于 "pill"（层次闸门按精确匹配找） */
  function statusPill(text) {
    return UI ? UI.pill(text) : el("span", { class: "pill", text: text })
  }

  /** 数字块 */
  function statGrid(items) {
    if (UI) return UI.statGrid(items)
    return el("div", { class: "stat-grid" }, items.map(function (item) {
      return el("div", { class: "stat" }, [
        el("span", { class: "stat-value", text: String(item.value) }),
        el("span", { class: "stat-label", text: String(item.label) }),
      ])
    }))
  }

  /** 主卡：现在该做什么 */
  function heroPanel(gaps, onReview) {
    const children = [el("h2", { text: "下一步" })]
    if (gaps.length === 0) {
      children.push(el("p", { class: "reason", text: "供给上没有缺口：三轨的内容都齐了。" }))
      return UI ? UI.hero({ title: "下一步", children: children.slice(1) }) : el("section", { class: "panel hero" }, children)
    }
    const counts = {}
    gaps.forEach(function (gap) {
      ;(gap.missing || []).forEach(function (kind) {
        counts[kind] = (counts[kind] || 0) + 1
      })
    })
    children.push(el("p", {
      class: "reason",
      text: "还差 " + gaps.length + " 处：缺断言脚本 " + (counts.asserts || 0)
        + " 个、缺讲解正文 " + (counts.statement || 0)
        + " 个、缺测试用例 " + (counts.cases || 0)
        + " 个、缺参考解法 " + (counts.solution || 0) + " 个。",
    }))
    children.push(el("p", {
      class: "muted",
      text: "补缺口在命令行里跑：node tools/supply.js generate --track database",
    }))
    children.push(el("div", { class: "row" }, [
      el("button", { class: "primary", text: "抽检第一个缺口", onclick: function () { onReview(gaps[0], true) } }),
      el("button", { text: "打回第一个缺口", onclick: function () { onReview(gaps[0], false) } }),
    ]))
    return UI ? UI.hero({ title: "下一步", children: children.slice(1) }) : el("section", { class: "panel hero" }, children)
  }

  /** 各轨进度 */
  function trackPanel(tracks) {
    const totals = { todo: 0, draft: 0, verified: 0, rejected: 0 }
    tracks.forEach(function (item) {
      totals.todo += Number(item.todo || 0)
      totals.draft += Number(item.draft || 0)
      totals.verified += Number(item.verified || 0)
      totals.rejected += Number(item.rejected || 0)
    })
    const items = [
      { label: "待补内容", value: totals.todo + " 个" },
      { label: "待校验", value: totals.draft + " 个" },
      { label: "已验证", value: totals.verified + " 个" },
      { label: "已打回", value: totals.rejected + " 个" },
    ]
    const children = [statGrid(items)]
    tracks.forEach(function (item) {
      const code = String(item.track || "")
      children.push(el("div", { class: "kv" }, [
        el("div", {}, [
          document.createTextNode((TRACK_LABELS[code] || code) + "："),
          el("span", { text: "共 " + item.total + " 个" }),
          statusPill("待校验 " + item.draft + " 个"),
          statusPill("已验证 " + item.verified + " 个"),
          statusPill("已打回 " + item.rejected + " 个"),
        ]),
      ]))
    })
    return UI ? UI.panel({ title: "各轨进度", children: children }) : el("section", { class: "panel" }, children)
  }

  /** 缺口清单 */
  function gapPanel(gaps, onReview) {
    const children = []
    if (gaps.length === 0) {
      children.push(el("p", { class: "muted", text: "没有缺口。" }))
      return UI ? UI.panel({ title: "缺口清单", children: children }) : el("section", { class: "panel" }, children)
    }
    gaps.forEach(function (gap) {
      const kinds = (gap.missing || []).map(function (kind) {
        return GAP_LABELS[kind] || kind
      })
      children.push(el("div", { class: "kv" }, [
        el("div", {}, [
          document.createTextNode(String(gap.id || "") + "　"),
          el("span", { text: String(gap.title || "") }),
        ]),
        el("div", { class: "row" }, [
          statusPill("缺：" + kinds.join("、")),
          el("button", { class: "primary", text: "通过", onclick: function () { onReview(gap, true) } }),
          el("button", { text: "打回", onclick: function () { onReview(gap, false) } }),
        ]),
      ]))
    })
    return UI ? UI.panel({ title: "缺口清单", children: children }) : el("section", { class: "panel" }, children)
  }

  /** 背景信息：运行状态与提醒 */
  function quietPanel(warnings) {
    const children = []
    if (warnings.length === 0) {
      children.push(el("p", { class: "muted", text: "没有提醒。" }))
    } else {
      warnings.forEach(function (text) {
        children.push(el("p", { class: "muted", text: text }))
      })
    }
    return UI
      ? UI.panel({ title: "运行状态与提醒", children: children, className: "quiet" })
      : el("section", { class: "panel quiet" }, children)
  }

  /**
   * 把接口返回的数据变成一棵界面树。纯函数，方便测。
   * @param {object} status
   * @returns {HTMLElement[]}
   */
  function buildSupplyView(status) {
    const data = status || {}
    const tracks = Array.isArray(data.tracks) ? data.tracks : []
    const gaps = Array.isArray(data.gaps) ? data.gaps : []
    const warnings = Array.isArray(data.warnings) ? data.warnings : []
    const nodes = []
    const notice = el("p", { class: "muted", text: "" })

    function onReview(gap, passed) {
      const id = String((gap && gap.id) || "")
      if (id === "") return
      notice.textContent = "正在写回 " + id + "……"
      A.postJson("/api/supply/review/" + encodeURIComponent(id), { passed: passed, note: "" })
        .then(function () {
          notice.textContent = "已写回 " + id + (passed ? "：通过" : "：打回")
        })
        .catch(function (err) {
          notice.textContent = "写回失败：" + String((err && err.message) || err)
        })
    }

    if (UI) {
      nodes.push(UI.pageHead({
        eyebrow: "供给",
        title: "供给状态",
        sub: "缺口是「还没补齐的东西」，抽检是「人看过之后给个结论」。两个动作都写回 learning 仓的单元文件。",
      }))
    }
    nodes.push(heroPanel(gaps, onReview))
    nodes.push(trackPanel(tracks))
    nodes.push(gapPanel(gaps, onReview))
    nodes.push(notice)
    nodes.push(quietPanel(warnings))
    return nodes
  }

  A.pages.supply = {
    title: "供给",
    render: async function (view) {
      view.textContent = ""
      view.appendChild(el("div", { class: "panel", text: "正在看供给状态…" }))
      let status = null
      try {
        status = await A.getJson("/api/supply/status")
      } catch (err) {
        view.textContent = ""
        view.appendChild(el("section", { class: "panel" }, [
          el("h2", { text: "加载失败" }),
          el("p", { class: "error", text: String((err && err.message) || err) }),
        ]))
        return
      }
      view.textContent = ""
      buildSupplyView(status).forEach(function (node) {
        view.appendChild(node)
      })
    },
  }

  // Node（单测）下导出纯函数，老测试直接用同一份实现
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) {
    module.exports = {
      buildSupplyView: buildSupplyView,
      statusPill: statusPill,
      GAP_LABELS: GAP_LABELS,
      TRACK_LABELS: TRACK_LABELS,
      STATUS_LABELS: STATUS_LABELS,
    }
  }
})()
```

```js
  // P4：供给接口要看到「单元现在长什么样」，这里加载一次单元列表。

  }
```

### Task 10: 端到端（`tests/supply-e2e.test.js`）

**Files:**
- 新建 `tests/supply-e2e.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `tools/supply.js`（**Task 8 产出**）：`runCli(argv, ctx) : Promise<{code, stdout, stderr}>`
  - `core/supply/verify.js`（**Task 7 产出**）：`verifyUnit(params)`、`SUPPLY_STATUSES`
  - `core/supply/unit-file.js`（**Task 4 产出**）：`unitFilePath(unitsDir, unit)`
  - `core/unit/index.js`（**P0 交付**）：`loadUnits(opts)`
  - `core/llm/service.js`（**Task 5 产出**）：`createLlmService(opts)`
- Produces: 无（这一期只补测试，不产出新的运行期接口）

**四个场景**：

| 场景 | 做什么 | 断言 |
|---|---|---|
| **A** | 一张 DB 卡 `draft → verified`：执行器原样跑通过 + 变异测试通过 | 单元文件 `status: verified`；learning 仓**不多出任何文件**（G18） |
| **B** | 恒真断言（改坏了也看不出来）→ `rejected` | 单元文件 `status: rejected`；理由含「变异测试未捕获，断言无效」 |
| **C** | LLM 关掉时 `plan` / `verify` / `report` 照常可用（G10） | 三个子命令退出码都是 0，且 `plan` 打出缺口、`report` 打出计数 |
| **D** | 跑完之后 learning 仓工作树**一个文件都不多**（G18，`mvcc_demo.db` 这类污染必须杜绝） | `git status --porcelain -z --untracked-files=all` 里除单元文件外没有任何新路径；**没有** `.db` 文件 |

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败测试**

新建 `tests/supply-e2e.test.js`：

```js
"use strict"
/**
 * supply-e2e.test.js —— 供给流水线端到端
 *
 * 四个场景：
 *   A. 一张 DB 卡 draft → verified（执行器原样跑通过 + 变异测试抓到改坏）
 *   B. 恒真断言 → draft → rejected（变异测试没抓到）
 *   C. LLM 关掉时 plan / verify / report 照常可用（G10）
 *   D. 跑完之后 learning 仓工作树一个文件都不多（G18）
 *
 * 全程不联网（G12）：LLM 客户端是假客户端。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")
const { execFileSync } = require("node:child_process")

const { runCli, EXIT_OK, EXIT_LLM_OFF } = require("../tools/supply.js")
const { resolveToolchain } = require("../judge/sandbox.js")

const TOOLCHAIN = resolveToolchain({})
const HAS_PYTHON = Boolean(TOOLCHAIN.python)
const SKIP = HAS_PYTHON ? false : "本机没有可用的 Python 解释器，跑不了真实执行"

/** 契约 §3.5 冻结的断言脚本模板（新版：subprocess + 临时目录 + 不写死 text=True）——
 *  与契约里的模板**逐字一致**，只把 <stem> 换成 demo、CHECKS 里的一条换成
 *  「算对了」。三条硬性约定都在里面：
 *    ① demo 用 subprocess 跑，不 import；
 *    ② 脚本自己把 demo 复制进 tempfile.mkdtemp() 造的临时目录再跑，跑完 rmtree；
 *    ③ 不写死 text=True——抓字节，再按 utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 依次试解码。 */
const SCRIPT_TEXT = [
  "#!/usr/bin/env python3",
  '"""demo 卡断言脚本 —— 校验 demo 的关键结论都出现在输出里。',
  "",
  "用法：",
  "  1) 直接跑：把本脚本与 demo 的 main.py 放在同一目录（沙箱副本）。",
  "  2) 指定 demo：环境变量 STUDY_HUB_DEMO 指向 demo 的绝对路径。",
  "全部断言通过 → exit 0；任一条不通过 → 打印失败原因并 exit 1。",
  "",
  "编码：demo 的 stdout 按**本机 locale** 编码（Windows 中文环境是 GBK，Linux 是 UTF-8），",
  "所以这里抓字节再按 utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 的顺序试解码，不写死编码。",
  '"""',
  "",
  "import os",
  "import shutil",
  "import subprocess",
  "import sys",
  "import tempfile",
  "",
  'DEMO = os.environ.get("STUDY_HUB_DEMO") or os.path.join(',
  '    os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "main.py"',
  ")",
  "",
  "# (说明, 在 stdout 里必须出现的片段)",
  "CHECKS = [",
  '    ("算对了", "[[OK]] 算对了"),',
  "]",
  "",
  "",
  "def decode(raw):",
  '    """把字节流解成文本：utf-8 → gbk → latin-1 依次试。"""',
  '    for enc in ("utf-8", "gbk"):',
  "        try:",
  "            return raw.decode(enc)",
  "        except UnicodeDecodeError:",
  "            continue",
  '    return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")',
  "",
  "",
  "def run_demo():",
  '    """把 demo 复制进临时目录再跑。',
  "",
  "    为什么要复制：这几个 demo 会在 __file__ 旁边写 .db 文件，而且不能重复跑。",
  "    复制进临时目录，既保证每次都是干净环境，也保证 .db 不落进 learning 仓。",
  '    """',
  "    if not os.path.exists(DEMO):",
  '        return None, "找不到 demo：" + DEMO',
  '    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="study-hub-assert-")',
  "    try:",
  "        target = os.path.join(sandbox, os.path.basename(DEMO))",
  "        shutil.copy2(DEMO, target)",
  "        proc = subprocess.run(",
  "            [sys.executable, target],",
  "            capture_output=True,",
  "            timeout=180,",
  "        )",
  "        return proc, None",
  "    finally:",
  "        shutil.rmtree(sandbox, ignore_errors=True)",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  "    proc, err = run_demo()",
  "    if err is not None:",
  "        print(err)",
  "        return 1",
  "    out = decode(proc.stdout)",
  "    failed = []",
  "    for name, needle in CHECKS:",
  "        if needle not in out:",
  '            failed.append(name + "（缺少：" + needle + "）")',
  "    if proc.returncode != 0:",
  '        failed.append("demo 退出码非 0：" + str(proc.returncode))',
  '        failed.append("demo 的 stderr 末尾：" + decode(proc.stderr)[-500:])',
  "    if failed:",
  "        for item in failed:",
  '            print("断言失败：" + item)',
  "        return 1",
  '    print("全部断言通过（%d 条）" % len(CHECKS))',
  "    return 0",
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    sys.exit(main())",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 有分支、有返回值：变异改坏后输出会变，断言抓得住 */
const DEMO_MUTABLE = [
  "def compute(x):",
  "    return x + 1",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  "    value = compute(1)",
  "    if value == 2:",
  '        print("[[OK]] 算对了")',
  "    else:",
  '        print("[[FAIL]] 算错了")',
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    main()",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 恒定输出：改坏了也看不出来 → 断言无效（有变异点，但变异后输出不变） */
const DEMO_VACUOUS = [
  "def compute(x):",
  "    return x + 1",
  "",
  "",
  "def main():",
  '    print("[[OK]] 算对了")',
  "",
  "",
  'if __name__ == "__main__":',
  "    main()",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** loader C 的单元文件 */
const UNIT_FILE = [
  "---",
  "id: db:demo",
  "track: database",
  "kind: card",
  "title: 演示卡",
  "difficulty: medium",
  "topics: []",
  "source: generated",
  "language: python",
  "demo: scaffold/grokking/demo/main.py",
  'reference: ""',
  "graded: script",
  "script: units/database/demo.assert.py",
  "status: draft",
  "reviewed: false",
  'reviewed_at: ""',
  "subtracks: []",
  "---",
  "",
  "## 讲解",
  "",
  "演示卡的讲解正文。",
  "",
  "## 关联",
  "",
  "- 轮次：R37 Grokking 数据库",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 造一个假的 learning 仓 */
function makeRepo(demoText) {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-e2e-repo-"))
  execFileSync("git", ["init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.email", "t@t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["config", "user.name", "t"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const cardDir = path.join(root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo")
  fs.mkdirSync(cardDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "main.py"), demoText, "utf8")
  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units")
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database"), { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.assert.py"), SCRIPT_TEXT, "utf8")
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), UNIT_FILE, "utf8")
  execFileSync("git", ["add", "-A"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  execFileSync("git", ["commit", "-m", "init"], { cwd: root, stdio: "ignore" })
  const stateDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "supply-e2e-state-"))
  return { root: root, unitsDir: unitsDir, stateFile: path.join(stateDir, "self-written.json") }
}

/** learning 仓工作树里现在多了哪些文件（NUL 切分，G14 的同一套口径） */
function strayPaths(root) {
  const out = execFileSync(
    "git",
    ["-c", "core.quotepath=false", "status", "--porcelain", "-z", "--untracked-files=all"],
    { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" }
  )
  return out.split("\0").filter(function (s) { return s !== "" })
}

/** 跑一次 CLI */
function cli(argv, ctx) {
  const base = {
    env: {},
    learningRoot: null,
    localBank: null,
    dbPath: ":memory:",
    stateFile: null,
    client: null,
    ask: null,
  }
  return runCli(argv, Object.assign(base, ctx || {}))
}

test("场景 A：DB 卡 draft → verified（断言通过 + 变异被捕获）", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE)
  const res = await cli(["verify", "--unit", "db:demo"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(res.code, EXIT_OK, res.stderr)
  assert.ok(res.stdout.indexOf("已验证") !== -1, res.stdout)
  const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo.unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), "utf8")
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("status: verified") !== -1, text)
})

test("场景 B：恒真断言 → rejected（变异测试未捕获，断言无效）", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_VACUOUS)
  const res = await cli(["verify", "--unit", "db:demo"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(res.code, EXIT_OK, res.stderr)
  assert.ok(res.stdout.indexOf("已打回") !== -1, res.stdout)
  assert.ok(res.stdout.indexOf("变异测试未捕获，断言无效") !== -1, res.stdout)
  const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo.unitsDir, "database", "demo.md"), "utf8")
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("status: rejected") !== -1, text)
})

test("场景 C：LLM 关掉时 plan / verify / report 照常可用（G10）", async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE)
  const off = {
    name: "null",
    model: "null",
    reason: "没有配置 API Key",
    complete: async function () { return "" },
  }
  const plan = await cli(["plan", "--track", "database"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
    client: off,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(plan.code, EXIT_OK, plan.stderr)
  assert.ok(plan.stdout.indexOf("供给缺口清单") !== -1, plan.stdout)

  const report = await cli(["report"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
    client: off,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(report.code, EXIT_OK, report.stderr)
  assert.ok(report.stdout.indexOf("供给报告") !== -1, report.stdout)

  const verify = await cli(["verify", "--all"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
    client: off,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(verify.code, EXIT_OK, verify.stderr)

  const generate = await cli(["generate", "--track", "database"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
    client: off,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(generate.code, EXIT_LLM_OFF, generate.stderr)
})

test("场景 D：跑完之后 learning 仓工作树一个文件都不多（G18）", { skip: SKIP }, async function () {
  const repo = makeRepo(DEMO_MUTABLE)
  const demoFile = path.join(repo.root, "scaffold", "grokking", "demo", "main.py")
  const before = fs.readFileSync(demoFile, "utf8")
  const res = await cli(["verify", "--all"], {
    learningRoot: repo.root,
    stateFile: repo.stateFile,
  })
  assert.strictEqual(res.code, EXIT_OK, res.stderr)
  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(demoFile, "utf8"), before, "learning 仓里的 demo 一个字节都不许动")

  const strays = strayPaths(repo.root)
  strays.forEach(function (item) {
    assert.strictEqual(/\.db$/i.test(item), false, "learning 仓里不许多出 .db 文件：" + item)
  })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(strays, [" M units/database/demo.md"], "只该多出单元文件这一处改动：" + strays.join("、"))
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑测试，确认结果**

```bash
cd projects/apps/study-hub && node tests/supply-e2e.test.js
```

**Expected（通过）**（**实跑**出来的数字，毫秒数会有出入，条目与条数必须一致）：

```
✔ 场景 A：DB 卡 draft → verified（断言通过 + 变异被捕获） (604.5858ms)
✔ 场景 B：恒真断言 → rejected（变异测试未捕获，断言无效） (573.4713ms)
✔ 场景 C：LLM 关掉时 plan / verify / report 照常可用（G10） (580.5389ms)
✔ 场景 D：跑完之后 learning 仓工作树一个文件都不多（G18） (615.8128ms)
ℹ tests 4
ℹ suites 0
ℹ pass 4
ℹ fail 0
```

> **本机没有 Python 时**：场景 A / B / D 会被跳过（显示 `ℹ skipped 3`），场景 C 照常通过。
>
> **场景 B 为什么是「恒真断言」而不是「没有可改点」**：demo 里那句 `return x + 1` 就是一个**变异点**——
> 把它改坏成 `return None`，demo 的输出一点没变（`main` 压根没调用 `compute`），
> 于是断言脚本照样通过 → 变异测试抓不住 → 判定「断言无效」，卡被打回。
> 这正是「断言没真的盯着被测逻辑」的样子；**「整个 demo 一个可改点都没有」是另一种失败**
> （`core/supply/verify.js` 给的理由是「找不到可变异点，无法验证断言有效性」），
> 那一条由 `tests/supply-verify.test.js`（Task 7）单独钉住。
